# LANShare - Project Handover

> **READ FIRST - CHANGE LOG RULE (owner, 2026-10-26) - do this in EVERY session that changes the app, before you zip**
> - The app's **update dialog shows a change log BEFORE the download button** (what was added / removed / changed, for every version the user skipped). It comes from **`CHANGELOG.md`** in the repo root: CI (`build-apk.yml`, step *Release notes*) publishes the **TOP `## ` section** of that file as the GitHub release body, and `UpdateCheck.kt` (`changeView` / `notesText`) renders it (green **Added**, red **Removed**, accent for Changed / Fixed).
> - **Session checklist (never skip):** (1) add a NEW section at the TOP of `CHANGELOG.md` (below the note lines): `## YYYY-MM-DD - short title`, then `### Added` / `### Removed` / `### Changed` / `### Fixed` with `- ` bullets, plain words for the owner, one line per visible change, no file names; (2) add the developer-level line to section 14 of this file as before; (3) only then zip. A session that changed the app but did not touch `CHANGELOG.md` is **not finished**.
> - Never edit older sections of `CHANGELOG.md`. Only the top section is published; if nobody adds a new top section, CI warns and the release says "Changes not listed for this build." (identical text to the previous release is treated the same, so a stale zip cannot re-publish old notes).
> - Docs-only or tooling-only sessions (nothing the user sees) need no new section.

> **READ FIRST - standing rule from the owner (2026-10-22)**
> - **Token economy.** All coding is done on *free* Claude accounts, so tokens are scarce. Do not waste them: read only the files you need (grep / `sed -n` ranges, never `cat` whole big files), no long explanations or repeated summaries, patch with small scripted edits instead of rewriting files, no unrequested extras, keep replies short.

- Start at section 1 (what the project is) and section 2 (status board), then jump to the chapter of the area you are working on. Every chapter has the same shape: *Summary, Files, How it works, Limits / not done, Test checklist*.
- Add new work to the **chapter of its area**, one line to the **developer changelog** (section 14) and the user-visible lines to **`CHANGELOG.md`** (shown in the update dialog; rule at the very top). Do not add new top-level `HANDOVER_*.md` files and do not stack dated notes at the top of this file.

## 1. Project at a glance

**What it is.** LANShare is an Android file manager that browses and moves files between this phone, other LANShare phones, SMB / FTP / SFTP servers and a Windows PC on the same network, and also prints, shares text, searches pictures with AI and analyses storage. Pure Kotlin; no Python any more.

**Architecture (one picture in words)**
- **Kotlin core** (`core/*.kt`): an HTTP server (`MiniHttp`, a port from the 8765-8784 range in the Python era - check `Core`) with routes in `Routes.kt`, a job engine (`Jobs`), file systems behind one interface (`Endpoint`: `LocalFs`, `RemoteFs`, `SmbFs`, `FtpFs`, `SftpFs`, `RootFs`, `ArchiveFs`), UDP/TCP discovery (`Discovery`), config in `<filesDir>/lanshare.json` (`Cfg`).
- **UI** = `app/src/main/assets/ui.html` (one large page, ~470 KB) in a `WebView`, plus native Kotlin views painted on top for speed (native file list, path bar, chrome, drawer). `MainActivity.Bridge` is the JS <-> Kotlin interface (`LSAndroid.*`). Quick local GETs are answered in-process (`InProc`) instead of over TCP.
- **Native screens** (plain `Activity`, no Compose, no AppCompat): video player, image viewer / editor, PDF viewer, text editor, Office viewer, Storage Analyzer, Inbox (text sharing).
- **Foreground service** `LanShareService` keeps the process, server and discovery alive; wake / Wi-Fi / multicast locks are held once in `Core.start` (`holdAwake`).
- **PC side** (Windows, Python): `pcprint.py` (print + text inbox service on port 8799, with a small Tk window) and `tools/phone_explorer.py` (opens the phone's FTP share in Explorer; built to an .exe by `build-phone-explorer-exe.yml`).

**Stack and build**
- Gradle (AGP 8.5.2, Kotlin 1.9.24), `minSdk 24`, `targetSdk 34`, ABI filter `arm64-v8a` only (ONNX Runtime + ML Kit took the APK from ~8 MB to ~110 MB before the filter).
- **Editions (2026-10-29):** CI builds TWO APKs from one commit IN PARALLEL (`build-apk.yml`: job `prep` = version + shared versionCode, matrix job `build` over `edition: [full, lite]` = two runners, job `release` = downloads both artifacts, one GitHub release with both APKs; `fail-fast: false`, release only if both pass) and attaches both to the release: `LANShare-v<ver>.apk` (full) and `LANShare-v<ver>-lite.apk` (`gradle -Pedition=lite`). Lite has its own applicationId `com.lanshare.app.lite` (`applicationIdSuffix`, label "LANShare Lite" via manifest placeholder `appLabel`) so BOTH can be installed side by side; data / settings / Drive sign-in are NOT shared; both use the same ports (2121 / 41234 / 41235 / MiniHttp), so run one at a time. Same signing key and the SAME versionCode (computed once in the *Sürüm* step, passed as `VERSION_CODE`); because the applicationId differs they do NOT replace each other (the shared versionCode only keeps the numbers in step); lite adds `-lite` to versionName. Lite leaves out `liteExclude` (top of `app/build.gradle.kts`: `fonts/office/**`, `fonts/ff_*.ttf`, `fonts/tnr-*`, `fonts/urbanist-*`; Figtree stays) by deleting those files from the merged assets (`merge*Assets` `doLast`; the source tree is untouched) and `BuildConfig.LITE` is true: `Fnt.LIST` = Figtree + System only, `OfficeViewerActivity.fontUrl` = null (no `@font-face`). `UpdateCheck.pickApk` downloads only the asset of its own edition (`-lite.apk` suffix). CI fails if a lite APK still holds `assets/fonts/office/` or `ff_*`, and prints both sizes in the run summary. To trim more in lite: add patterns to `liteExclude` and guard the feature with `BuildConfig.LITE`. ONNX runtime (image search + PaddleOCR) is ALSO out of lite (2026-10-29): `compileOnly` in lite, so only `ClipEngine.kt` / `PaddleOcr.kt` (the only files importing `ai.onnxruntime`) compile against it; every path that could create `ClipEngine.Impl` / `PaddleOcr` is guarded by `BuildConfig.LITE` (`ClipEngine.tryLoad/install/uninstall/installed`, `PaddleModels.installed/installAsync`, `AutoOcr.recognize` = ML Kit only); `ClipEngine.state()` carries `lite:true` so ui.html shows a note instead of the Download button. CI fails if the lite APK holds `libonnxruntime`. NEW code touching `ClipEngine.Impl`, `PaddleOcr`, `OrtSession` must be guarded the same way, else lite throws NoClassDefFoundError at runtime. Not compiled / not device-tested.
- CI `.github/workflows/build-apk.yml`: builds on push to `main` / `master` and on manual dispatch (dispatch also creates a release + tag). Version = `VERSION` (major, now `2`) + next free tag number; `versionCode` = minutes since 2026-01-01 UTC; signed release only when the repo secret `KEYSTORE_B64` (+ `KEYSTORE_PASSWORD`, `KEY_ALIAS`, `KEY_PASSWORD`) exists. Keystore: `keytool -genkey -v -keystore release.jks -alias lanshare -keyalg RSA -keysize 2048 -validity 10000`, then `base64 -w0 release.jks` into the secret.
- Dependencies worth knowing: ONNX Runtime Android (CLIP + PaddleOCR), ML Kit text recognition (unbundled Play Services artifact), Media3 / ExoPlayer 1.3.1 (player), `viewpager2`, `recyclerview` 1.3.2, `swiperefreshlayout`, smbj (SMB), commons-compress 1.21 (kept at 1.21 for minSdk 24) + xz 1.9 (archives), zxing core 3.5.3 (QR), core-ktx 1.13.1. **No** Apache POI / PDF library / Compose by design.
- Language: UI text is English with a few Turkish strings (e.g. the update dialog "Bu sürümü atla"). Older handovers were partly Turkish; this one is English.

**Wire protocol** is unchanged from the Python era: old Python builds and Kotlin builds interoperate (`/p/*` routes between devices, UDP beacon port 48555).

## 4. Working conventions

- **Delivery**: the project was developed in short chat sessions with no Android SDK, so every change is delivered as files to copy over the repo (paths are repo-relative). Storage Analyzer parts were delivered as the FULL app zip without `.git`, `build/`, `.gradle/`; for other work the user prefers a **minimal zip with only the changed files**. Ask if unsure.
- **Change log (every session)**: new top section in `CHANGELOG.md` for anything the user can see - see the READ FIRST rule at the top of this file. It is what the update dialog shows before the download.
- **Honesty flags**: every note says what was compiled / run and what was not. Keep doing that. "JS syntax-checked with node", "braces balanced by script", "mirror test" are weaker than a build.
- **Build loop**: push -> Actions build -> paste the compiler errors -> fix. CI only builds on `main` / `master` push or manual dispatch, so a feature branch shows errors only after merge or a dispatch.
- **Branch / PR**: extensive commit message and PR description (what and why). End commits with the `Co-Authored-By` / `Claude-Session` trailers of the current session and the PR body with the "Generated with Claude Code" line + session URL (use the ones the session provides). Give the compare link after pushing: `https://github.com/topgun1111/pcshare/compare/main...<branch>`.
- **Session size**: work in small parts (one part per session for big features), attach only the files named for the part, update this file at the end.
- **JVM-testable core**: `core/office/*` has no `android.*` imports on purpose; compile with `kotlinc 2.0.21` (see 8.2). Algorithms with a Python mirror: `tools/storage_scan_mirror_test.py`.
- **Kill switches / flags** (use them to bisect a bad build): `InProc.enabled`, `NativeList.HEAD` / `ls_nlh='0'`, `NlChrome.ENABLED` / `ls_nlc='0'`, `NlDrawer.ENABLED` / `ls_nld='0'`, `TiledOcr.skipTilesIfEmpty`, `ENGINE_ID` (bump to discard the image index).
- **Gotcha list for Kotlin**: `org.json` drops a key on `put(k, null)` (use `JSONObject.NULL`); never call `removeFirst()/removeLast()` on a `java.util.List` below Android 15 (only on `kotlin.collections.ArrayDeque`); KDoc must not contain `/*` (it nests - an `_rels/*.rels` comment once broke a whole file).
- **PC helper build**: `build-phone-explorer-exe.yml` builds `tools/phone_explorer.py` to an .exe; `pcprint.py` is run directly with Python on the PC.

## 5. Platform core (server, protocol, jobs, file systems)

### 5.1 Server, routes and behaviour gotchas
**Summary.** `core/*.kt` serves `assets/ui.html` from `MiniHttp` and answers the `/api/*` and `/p/*` routes (`Routes.kt`). The wire protocol equals the old Python server, so builds interoperate. The Chaquopy / Python era is gone (history in 5.12).

**Gotchas to keep in mind**
- `org.json` **drops a key** on `put(k, null)`: real nulls must be `JSONObject.NULL` (used for `used`, `storage_ok`, `error`, clipboard).
- Query parsing mimics Python `parse_qs`: `+` / `%XX` decoded, **blank values dropped**, first value wins.
- `MiniHttp` does not support chunked request bodies, `Expect: 100-continue` or `HEAD` (the Python server had none either). An error mid-upload closes the connection instead of draining the body.
- Android < 15: never call `removeFirst()/removeLast()` on a `java.util.List`; only on `kotlin.collections.ArrayDeque`.
- `storageOk` is evaluated live on every call. `File.renameTo` is used for rename / move, with copy + delete fallback across volumes (`LocalFs.move`).
- `tcpProbe`: "connection refused" = host up, try the next port; anything else = nobody home (same as Python's errno logic).
- The app's HTTP server answers **any app on the phone** at `127.0.0.1`; anything sensitive (root, 5.6) needs a per-process token.
- Port: the server tries a range (8765-8784 in the Python era); the URL is read back from `Core.url`, so any port works. `Core.url` is published at ~106 ms after process start (13).
- Config: `<filesDir>/lanshare.json` (`Cfg`). SMB passwords are stored in plain text in the app's private storage.

**Discovery** (`Discovery.kt`): UDP beacons (port 48555) + unicast sweep + TCP scan + live checks. Network changes (`ConnectivityManager` default-network callback in `LanShareService`) trigger a debounced rescan.

**Security note**: no PIN / pairing by design; use on trusted Wi-Fi. An opt-in PIN / trusted-networks toggle was never built. `MANAGE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE` is fine for sideloading, needs Play justification otherwise.

### 5.2 In-process API (`core/InProc.kt`)
`MainActivity.shouldInterceptRequest` answers the WebView's quick local GETs `/api/ls`, `/api/space`, `/api/stat` (only `dev=local`, no `!` archive paths), `/api/job`, `/api/clip`, `/api/info` plus later additions (`/api/peers`, `/api/smb` GET, `/api/arcjob`, `/api/sscanp`, `/api/sscannode`, `/api/sscantop`, `/api/jobs`) by running `Routes.handle` against an in-memory `Exchange`: byte-identical answers, no TCP / thread / HTTP parse. POSTs, `/api/dl`, thumbnails, other devices, SMB, archives, search and any 5xx return `null` and go the HTTP way (WebViewClient cannot see POST bodies). Kill switch `InProc.enabled = false`; answers slower than 300 ms are logged as `LANShare: slow in-process ...`.
Other speed work: `LocalFs.ls` stats folders with >= 150 entries on 4 threads (`statPool`); `Routes ls` lower-cases names once; `ui.html` `prefetch` / `idlePre` warm the listing cache `LSC`, `thBusy < 4`, thumbnails use `/api/thumb` directly, selection toggles avoid a full `render()`, `touch-action: manipulation`. Not done on purpose: in-process thumbnails (would block intercept threads), counts, search, peers / SMB, a native listing cache (stale after other apps' changes).

### 5.3 Jobs, progress card and progress bubble
- `core/Jobs.kt`: every long task (copy, move, send, extract, zip, delete, print, archive listing, duplicate scan, storage scan) is a `Job` in `Jobs.all`, process-wide (survives Activity changes). `Job` carries `cur / curDone / curTotal / files / filesTotal / failed / dest / problems`; `toJson` sends them.
- **Detail card** in `ui.html` (`lvMake / lvFeed / lvEnd`, CSS `.pj`): verb + %, `from > to`, current file with its own bar, Transferred / Speed / Time left / Elapsed / Files n of m / Problems, live problem list, result summary. Opens by itself after 2 s only when the window is >= 400 x 520 px (never over another sheet); *Minimize* leaves the bubble. The merge-copy log and the "replace this file?" question live in the same card. `lvOn()` = card really on screen (it lives in `#sheet`, which ~25 other sheets reuse).
- **Bubble** `#jb` + panel `#jp` live OUTSIDE `#sheet` (fixed, z 21/22, above viewer 20): ring = average %, number = running jobs, `!` / tick when finished. Drag snaps to the nearest corner (`jbc` in localStorage, default top right), tap = panel with one row per job (bar, current file, files i/n, speed, Cancel; tap a row = its detail card; Clear; minimize; tap outside collapses); sized from the window (`min(340px, 100vw - 32px)`, 60 % of `--lvh`). `GET /api/jobs` lists running jobs + ones finished < 10 s ago; polled every 450 ms while something runs (`jbKick`). **Important**: the main file list is a native RecyclerView drawn over the WebView and only lets drawing / touches through "holes" listed in `nlLay()`; `#jb` / `#jp` are holes and `jbRender` / drag call `jbLay()` -> `nlLay(true)` + `nlChPush()`. If the bubble is missing: `chrome://inspect`, check `document.getElementById('jb').style.display` and `await api('GET','/api/jobs')`.
- **Native overlay for screens without a WebView** (`ops/JobView.kt`, `ops/JobOverlay.kt`): read-only rows from `Jobs.all`, cancel sets the same `Job.cancel` flag, finished rows linger 3 s / errors 10 s, bubble with ring, drag + snap (prefs `ops_overlay/corner`), card <= 340 dp, hidden in PiP and when nothing runs. `JobOverlay.install(this)` after `setContentView` in `ImageViewerActivity`, `PdfViewerActivity`, `TextEditorActivity`, `PlayerActivity` and `StorageActivity` (which hides its own `sscan:` job via `hidePrefix`); not in `MainActivity` (page card does it). The foreground notification of `LanShareService` shows running jobs (title = label or "N tasks running", progress bar). Origin: the `progress-overlay-handover-v4` package written for another app; only its missing view part was ported (not `OperationTracker` / `OperationsService`).
- Not done: hinge avoidance (needs androidx.window); notification tap opening the panel.

### 5.4 Resumable transfers (2026-10-18)
A copy / move that loses its link no longer starts the file again; an interrupted job can be continued later.
- **File level** (`Endpoint`): `resumable`, `partial(v, tag)`, `writeResume(v, input, size, offset, tag, cb)` (defaults = not resumable, so `RootFs` / archives unchanged). `LocalFs` and `SmbFs` write `name.lspart` as before but **keep** it when the transfer breaks (a user Cancel still removes it) and append from `offset` when continued. `RemoteFs` asks the other phone `GET /p/partial?path&tag` (answer `{have}`) and uploads the rest with `POST /p/put?path&tag&offset&total` (Content-Length = total - offset); a phone with no `partial` route answers "unknown route" and gets plain whole-file uploads.
- **Identity**: `tag` = `"size:mtime"` of the source; `core/Partials.kt` (`filesDir/partials.json`) stores the tag per `.lspart` (key = absolute path, or `smb|<id>|<share path>.lspart`), so a partial of an older / changed source is never reused and no sidecar files clutter folders. Entries and `.lspart` files older than 7 days are purged in `Core.start`.
- **Job level** (`Jobs.copyFile`): files >= 4 MB (`RESUME_MIN`) whose source can seek continue from the destination's `partial()`; retry with back-off 2 / 4 / 8 / 16 / 30 s ("Connection lost - retrying (n/6)", Cancel works while waiting); an attempt that gains >= 1 MB resets the failure count (cap 40 attempts); other files keep the old 4 tries. `Jobs.work` tracks unfinished top-level items and on an error saves a resume record (`Partials.putJob`: src / dst ids, dest folder, cut, items left, destination names the first run chose); `Jobs.resume(id)` re-runs `work` with conflict `"resume"` (same size = skipped and counted as done, different size = replaced from its `.lspart` when kept; folders keep the first run's names so a half-copied folder continues instead of becoming "name (1)"). Moves delete the original only when every file is complete.
- **API**: `GET /api/resumable`, `POST /api/jobresume?id` (-> `{job}`), `POST /api/resumedrop?id`; `Job.toJson` adds `resume` on an error. **UI**: failed copy card gets **Resume**; Settings > **Interrupted transfers** (only when something is left) lists records with Resume / Forget.
- **Tested** (JVM harness with stubs): `LocalFs.writeResume` + `partial` (keeps part, wrong offset rejected, changed source ignored, cancel removes, target untouched until complete, identical content) and the real `copyFile` against a flaky seekable source. **Not compiled / not device-tested**: `SmbFs.partial/writeResume`, `RemoteFs.partial/writeResume`, `Routes` put / partial, `Jobs.work` resume wiring, `ui.html` bits.
- Check: copy 500 MB phone -> phone, Wi-Fi off 20 s -> "Connection lost" then continues from the same MB; kill the receiving app mid-copy and copy the same folder again -> `.lspart` continues; copy a folder to SMB, pull the cable, Resume -> finished files skipped, no "(1)" folder; edit the source between tries -> restarts from 0.
- Not done: hash check of the kept part; Root / archive sources; a partial left by a Cancel is only purged after 7 days; single-file downloads by viewer / player unchanged.

### 5.5 SMB, FTP, SFTP
- **SMB** (`SmbFs.kt`, smbj): Settings > SMB shares; each saved share is a device that browses, searches, downloads, uploads, copies, renames, deletes and receives "Send to..." (lands in the share root). Use the PC's IP (no NetBIOS names); SMB1-only servers unsupported. Config `CFG["smb"]` = `{id, host, share, user, password, name}`; empty user = guest; the login is tested with `ls("/")` before saving. Optional **Share** field limits a PC to one share.
- **SMB screen** (`openSmb`): one card per saved PC (status dot, name, `\\host\share`, edit / remove with a 3 s "Remove?" second tap), found-on-network list with Rescan (`/api/smbscan`), one Add / Edit form. Edit = `POST /api/smb` with `edit:<id>` -> `Smb.update` (keeps id and position; empty password keeps the stored one; new login tested first; cached connection dropped; open tab reloads at `/`).
- **Duplicate finder on SMB**: `Endpoint.walkEach(v, visit)` streams the walk (reconnect-safe in `SmbFs`); `DupFinder` updates its label every 0.3 s and honours Cancel.
- **FTP / FTPS** (`FtpFs.kt`, saved under `"ftp"` in `lanshare.json`) and **SFTP** (`SftpFs.kt`, trust-on-first-use host key pinned in the saved server): remote files are read-only and seekable (REST + RETR, re-open at the current position after a drop); connections come from a borrowed pool (4 slots, `FtpPool`).
- **FTP listing speed (2026-10-22, 5 parts, Android side not compiled / device-tested)** - goal: folders open as fast as WinSCP / FileZilla.
  - **Cache** (`core/FtpCache.kt`, shared by FTP and SFTP): in-memory LRU (300 folders / 60,000 entries, fresh 30 s, TTL settable 0-3600 s); disk copy `filesDir/ftpcache/<sha1(server id)[0..16]>.json` (per server max 200 folders / 40,000 entries, entries older than 30 days ignored, saved 4 s after the last change). `ls()`: fresh hit = no connection; stale hit = returned at once while ONE background refresh runs (`ftp-refresh`); if the answer differs, `Core.onFtpChanged` -> `window.lsFtpChanged(dev, path)` reloads the open folder. Concurrent identical listings share one request (single-flight). Every write drops the folder, its parents and (remove / rename / move) the subtree (`FtpFs.dirty`). Never cached: `names()`, name-clash checks (rename / move), `fresh=1`.
  - **Protocol savings**: FEAT asked once per server (no MLSD in FEAT -> straight to LIST; with MLST `OPTS MLST type;size;modify;`), `SYST` remembered, symlink answers remembered (`FtpPool.links`), `TCP_NODELAY`. `entry()` (open / stat / walk root / remove) reads the parent's cached listing, else one `MLST <path>` on the control channel (failure falls back to the parent listing; 500-504 sets `FtpPool.noMlst`). Symlinks are resolved after the listing (`resolveLinks`, up to 200 per listing, 4 s budget, helper connections only while >= 2 slots are free). Search / walk / folder totals fill the cache (`listFill`, max 60 folders per operation, not when a write finished meanwhile).
  - **Connections**: `Endpoint.warmUp()` + `GET /api/warm?dev=` (called from `openDev` and on a fresh hit) logs in and parks a connection; keep-alive thread `ftp-keeper` (NOOP to idle connections older than 12 s, closes all idle ones 3 min after last use, `SO_KEEPALIVE`); **prefetch** after each `ls`: parent + first 6 sub-folders on a SPARE connection (only when >= 3 of 4 slots are free, one worker per server, stops when a tap queues); **FTPS session reuse** (one `SSLContext` per server, `ReuseSpi` / `ReuseFactory`, control-port session-cache key) for servers that require it.
  - **UI / tools**: pull-to-refresh sets `S.fresh=1` -> `&fresh=1` -> `Jobs.ep(dev).invalidate(path)`; `GET /api/ftpcache` (stats `{ttl, hits, stale, miss, folders, items, disk, log[]}`, `?clear=1`, `?ttl=`); FTP servers screen (`openFtp`) has a Listing cache block (stats, TTL button 0/10/30/120/600 s, Clear). `tools/ftp_cache_test.py`. SFTP does not log timings.
  - **Check**: open an FTP folder, force-close, Wi-Fi off, reopen -> list from disk; Wi-Fi on + a change from another client -> list reloads by itself; upload / rename / delete from the app -> no stale entry.
- `RemoteThumbs.kt`: thumbnails for other devices / SMB through the endpoint's `Source` (JPEG <= 400 px, disk cache keyed by dev + path + size + mtime), also used by the native list.
- **Google Drive** is the planned fourth remote kind (`gd:` ids, `GdriveFs`, saved under `"gdrive"` in `lanshare.json`): see chapter 17. `Jobs.ep` knows `gd:` since Part 4 (read-only until Part 5).
- No dedicated handover ever existed for FTP / SFTP beyond the above (TLS details, `PeTls` / FTPS option see 10.2).

### 5.6 Root support (2026-10-16)
Settings > **Root access** (off by default) shows the whole file system as one more place, **Root** (device id `root`): browse, open, copy / move / rename / new folder / delete / search / details / duplicate finder via `su`.
- `core/RootShell.kt`: `check()` = `su -c id`, per-command `su -c`, `q()` quoting, per-run `token`, `sign(url)`. `core/RootFs.kt`: `Endpoint` over `find` / `stat -c` / `cat` / `tail -c +N` / `mv` / `rm` / `mkdir` / `df`; written files keep owner + mode of the file they replace, a new file gets its folder's owner; delete is permanent and refuses whole system folders; shell-loop fallback when `find -exec +` is missing.
- Wiring: `Cfg.rootEnabled()` (`root_on`), `Jobs.ep("root")` -> `RootShell.fs()`, `/api/peers` adds Root only when on AND granted, `GET/POST /api/root` (`enable` asks su first, `disable`, `check`), `Core.start` re-checks su, ui.html Settings row (confirm dialog) + cookie.
- **Security**: root is gated by a random per-process token that only exists in the app: the page gets it from `LSAndroid.rootToken()` and keeps it in cookie `lsroot` (`rootCk()`); `Routes.handle` marks a request untrusted (`RootShell.untrusted`) unless it is loopback AND carries the token (cookie / header `x-ls-root` / query `rt`); `Jobs.ep("root")` and every `dev=root` query refuse untrusted requests; native viewers / player / print / share add `rt` with `RootShell.sign()`. `/p/` routes (other devices) never serve root.
- Not done: Storage Analyzer scan of `/` as root, fast root video thumbnails (each seek restarts `tail`), recycle bin for root deletes. **Not compiled / not device-tested.**
- Check on a rooted phone: turn on -> Magisk / KernelSU prompt -> "Root" in the device list; open /data/data, view a picture, copy a file out and in (owner of an overwritten app file must stay the app's), rename, new folder, delete a test folder; switch off -> tab disappears; another app (Termux `curl 127.0.0.1:8765/api/ls?dev=root\&path=/`) must get 403.

### 5.7 Archives, ZIP, ZIP editing
- **Big archives on other devices** (Batch 7): `ArcStore.index` reads only the ZIP end record + central directory with ranged reads (`RangeZip` on any seekable `Source`: `RemoteSource`, `SmbSource`) so listing is instant; odd layouts (`ZipFormat` error) fall back to a full download. Stored entries are served in place through `WindowSource`; deflated ones are inflated into the cache with a progress job. Long steps register a `Job` via `ArcProg`; the UI polls `GET /api/arcjob` and shows the progress toast with Cancel (`/api/jobcancel`; cancelling a listing returns HTTP 499 `cancelled`, the UI goes back to the folder holding the archive). Copy / extract of >= 8 files out of a ZIP on another device first fetches the archive once (`Jobs.pinArchives`).
- **Zip feature**: selection bar > **Zip** (not inside archives). `POST /api/zip {dev, paths, dir, name, replace}` -> `Jobs.startZip / zipWork` streams every file through `Zip64Writer` (data descriptors, ZIP64 only where needed, already-compressed types stored as level-0 deflate), writes `<name>.zip`; local = straight into the folder (`.lspart` -> rename), remote = built in cache then uploaded; Cancel removes the partial. No size limit except free space. `doZip` lists the destination and when `name.zip` exists asks **Replace / Keep both / Cancel** (`dlg` got an optional `alt` button; `finalName` keeps the exact name; the old file is only removed when the new ZIP is complete).
- **More formats open like folders, read-only (2026-10-19)**: 7z / cb7, TAR / cbt, TAR.GZ / tgz, TAR.BZ2 / tbz / tbz2, TAR.XZ / txz, single .gz / .bz2 / .xz (one entry), ZIP-based jar / war / aar / xpi / apks / xapk. `.apk` and office files are deliberately NOT archives. Code: `ArchiveFs.kt` (`arcFmt()`, `ArcIndex(fmt)`, `ArcIndex.unpack`, `ArcStore.otherExtract`), ui.html extension regexes, manifest "Open with" entries. RAR5 and password-protected 7z unsupported (RAR via junrar needs the whole file). TAR / compressed files have no index: a big .tar.gz is unpacked once to list and again to open one file.
- **Archive editing**: `core/ZipEdit.kt` edits zip / cbz in place (adds replace the old central directory and roll back on error; deletes copy the rest raw to a temp file + rename; same name = new added, then old removed; ZIP64 / RAR unsupported; JVM-tested incl. `unzip -t`). `core/ArcEdit.kt` (2026-10-19): 7z, TAR, TAR.GZ/TGZ, TAR.BZ2/TBZ, TAR.XZ/TXZ add / delete / new folder by rewriting to `<name>.edit.part` with commons-compress and renaming; the ZIP family (zip cbz jar war aar xpi apks xapk) uses `ZipEdit`. `ArcEp.editable()` decides by format; RAR and single-file gz / bz2 / xz are read-only with a clear message. Only archives on this phone. `Jobs`: "cut" into an archive = copy; bulk delete = one rewrite (`removeMany`). Route `/api/addzip`; ui.html: Delete, Paste, "Edit ZIP" menu (Add files picker, New folder, Paste, Extract all), `zipEd()` covers the new extensions. Job label "Adding to archive". Not compiled (ArcEdit).
- **Archive details (2026-10-29)**: strip of chips (type, size, files, % saved, password) under the tool row while inside an archive + full sheet on tap; `core/ZipInfo.kt` / `core/ArcInfo.kt`, `/api/arcinfo`, `arcRow()` / `doArcInfo()` in `ui.html`. Test: open a ZIP, a password ZIP, a .7z and a .tar.gz on a phone; the strip must not move the list (fixed height) and the native list must sit right under it.

### 5.8 Recycle bin (`core/Bin.kt`, redesign 2026-10-09)
`openBin()` (ui.html): fixed top bar with **Empty bin**, tap-to-select rows (Select all / Restore / Delete / Clear), rows drawn 80 at a time, progress bar with Cancel. Restore / delete / empty run as ONE background task (`Bin.start` -> `Bin.Task`, `POST /api/bin {op:restore|purge|empty|cancel, ids}` returns at once; `GET /api/bin?items=0` = progress). `Bin.purge` first renames the entry to `.del-<id>` (atomic, vanishes at once) and deletes afterwards; leftovers swept at start (`sweepDeleted`). `Bin.list/restore/purge/empty` no longer hold the global lock. Items are kept `Bin.KEEP_DAYS` days. Checked in Chromium with a mocked API (400 items).

### 5.9 Folder statistics, albums, media info
- `GET /api/folderstat?dev=local&path=` -> `{files, bytes, partial}` (`LocalFs.stat`: recursive, symlinks skipped, 8 s cap then `partial`, 1 min cache) feeds the home tiles. It is one of the two slow background scans (max 467 ms now, 13).
- `Albums.kt`: gallery albums (MediaStore query, folder-walk fallback); `Albums.total / fullSized` count full-sized pictures only (longest side >= 500 px, or >= 20 KB when unknown; no `thumbnail` / `cache` / `tmp` / sticker / hidden / Android / system folders, no `*thumb*`).
- `Media.kt`: photo dimensions + date taken, video / audio duration + resolution (best effort).
- **Media info in Details (2026-10-21)**: for video / audio, `Media.av` also calls `details()` which adds to the `/api/stat` JSON: `container` `{mime, fmt, bitrate}` (bitrate falls back to size / duration), `tracks` `[{kind: video|audio|text|other, mime, codec, profile?, bitrate?, lang?, w, h, fps, hdr (video), rate, ch (audio)}]` from `MediaExtractor.getTrackFormat`, and `tags` `{title, artist, albumartist, album, track, genre, year, composer, author}` from `MediaMetadataRetriever`. No new library (the MediaInfoLib / FFmpeg options were rejected: native size, and the easy `nextlib-mediainfo` AAR is GPL-3.0). `ui.html` `doDetails` shows Container / Video / Audio / Subtitles / Tags sections above a "File" heading (CSS `.dsec`). Limits: only local files and other LANShare phones with this version (SMB / FTP / SFTP / archive entries have no `Media.info`); `MediaExtractor` reads only what Android's codecs know (no DTS / TrueHD profile, no Dolby Vision profile, bit depth, chroma, or writing application); HDR comes from `color-transfer` (PQ / HLG) only; frame rate may be missing for some MKV files. Test: Details on an MP4 (H.264 + AAC), an MKV with 2 audio + subtitle tracks, an HDR phone video, an MP3 with tags, a corrupt video (must show the basic rows only, no error).

### 5.10 Update check (`UpdateCheck.kt`)
Compares the highest release tag with the installed `versionName` (`isNewer`, "-sha" suffix ignored): same or older = no dialog and no download (manual check toasts "Güncel: <version>"); newer = dialog **İndir / Sonra / Bu sürümü atla** (skip stores a `skip` tag, hides only the automatic prompt for that tag). A manual check ignores the 6 h limit and a skipped version. The old "reinstall latest APK" path is gone.

**Change log in the dialog (2026-10-26, NOT compiled, not seen on a device):** the offer dialog is now a scrollable panel (current version, then **Neler değişti**) built by `changeView` / `notesText` from the `body` of EVERY release newer than the installed version (newest first, 4000 chars max per release; a `null` body or no bullets = "(not listed)"), so a skipped version's notes are not lost. `body` is the top section of `CHANGELOG.md`, extracted by the *Release notes* step of `build-apk.yml` and passed to `softprops/action-gh-release` as `body_path` (no commit-back to the repo; identical-to-previous-release or bullet-less text becomes "Changes not listed for this build." plus a CI warning). Rendering: `### Added` green, `### Removed` red, other `###` headings in the accent colour, `- ` bullets as "•"; `# ` / `## ` lines are skipped. Test: bump nothing, push a change with a new top section, open Settings > Check for updates on an older build, confirm the lists show before **İndir**.

### 5.11 Model download links (`ModelLinks.kt`)
Daily HEAD check (Wi-Fi only, from `MainActivity.onResume`) of every PaddleOCR + CLIP model link; 4xx (not 408 / 429) from a check or a real download shows the "Model download link is dead" dialog (Copy details / Dismiss) in the main screen and both viewers until fixed. Fix the link in `PaddleModels.BASE/FILES` (`PaddleOcr.kt`) or `ClipEngine.BASE/FILES`.

### 5.12 History (superseded, kept for context)
- **Python / Chaquopy era**: Chaquopy 16 ran the unmodified `lanshare.py` in a foreground service with `jnius.py` and `android_main.py` shims; replaced by the pure-Kotlin core. Tailscale backup IPs were replaced by SMB shares (Batch 6); an RC4 patch for NTLM under Android's OpenSSL is no longer relevant.
- **Share sheet**: files shared into the app are staged in `<storage>/LANShare Shared/` and the app opens its "Send to..." device picker (Batch 5); `PcPrintService` also makes LANShare appear as a printer in Android's print dialog. Receive folder name: `LANShare Received`.
- **Batch 8 / 9 native browser trial** (`BrowserActivity.kt`) and **Batch 4 "Native" drawer button** were REMOVED on 2026-10-04 (WebView UI is the only browser; the native list of 6.2 replaced the idea). Rollback instructions are obsolete.
- Original release test checklist (still a good smoke test): two phones, both appear within ~5 s; browse, copy / cut / paste, send both ways; picker upload, download to /Download; hotspot host + client; Wi-Fi toggle -> devices reappear; share from Gallery -> picker -> delivered in `LANShare Received`; screen off 10 min -> still reachable.

---

## 6. UI layer (ui.html, native list, main menu, gallery)

### 6.1 How the UI is built
- `assets/ui.html` owns screens and logic (single page, ~470 KB, ~224 DOM nodes at rest, ~10 MB JS heap, main script runs in 31-53 ms). `MainActivity.Bridge` (`LSAndroid.*`) is the JS <-> Kotlin interface; `onNewIntent` -> `handleShare` handles share intents (`ACTION_SEND`, `ACTION_SEND_MULTIPLE`).
- **Twin rule**: whatever Kotlin paints natively (list rows, path bar, chrome, drawer, search row) has an unchanged HTML twin underneath. The twin is the fallback and the source of truth for taps: native taps come back as `nlOn(...)` / `nlCh(...)` / `nlDr(...)` and click the same HTML element. Row builders exist twice (`NlModel.rows` in Kotlin, `nlBuild` in ui.html) and their `sig` (signature) must stay equal or rows flicker - when you add a row field, change **both** and include it in `sig`.
- **Holes**: the native list is a RecyclerView over the WebView; it lets drawing and touches through only for the rects `nlLay()` reports (`#fab #toast #dlw`, open sheets, `#jb`, `#jp`). A new floating element must be added there. Overlays that hide the native list / chrome / search / swipe (`#gal`, `#pv`, `#home`) are in the same lists (`nlLay`, search push, `nlChPush`, swipe `blocked`).
- Theme: page tokens `--sel --cont --ac ...`; `AppPal.kt` mirrors the page palette (from `nlPalette()`) in memory + SharedPreferences so native screens (Storage Analyzer) use the app's theme. `nlTheme()` (theme picker + system listener) re-pushes palette, chrome, search and layout (a theme change used to leave the list half-themed; `NlHeadView.pal` setter now sets `dirty`).
- Testing UI-only changes: `node --check` of the extracted JS, Chromium with mocked `api()`; `chrome://inspect` on the device.

### 6.2 Native list, path bar, chrome, drawer (phases of "Option A")
The page stays HTML; Kotlin draws the heavy parts on top. **The long design notes lived in `HANDOVER_NATIVE_LIST.md` (sections 2b-2p), which is not in the repo** - this table is the only surviving record. Phase numbers 4, 5, 8, 9 are not mentioned in any note.

| Phase | What | Files / keys | Switch |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Folder rows drawn in a RecyclerView laid over the WebView, look copied from the CSS | `NativeList.kt`, `Bridge.nl*`, ui.html hooks | `nlUse` |
| 2a | Compact view | `NlRowView.compact`, layout JSON `cp` | |
| 2b | Large-thumbnail list (row 84) | `NlRowView.large`, layout JSON `lg` | |
| 3a | Grid view | `NlGridView`, `GridLayoutManager` 2 / 4 columns, palette `bg` | |
| 3b | Video gallery (`.gal`) | `NlGalView`, rows flagged `v:1` | |
| 6a | Folder entry no longer waits for the page: next folder's rows built in Kotlin (list, sort, dups, texts), page revalidates | `NlModel.kt`, `NativeList.tapRow` (JS `nlStash` removed) | |
| 7a | Path bar + tool row native | `NlHead.kt`, bridge `nlHead`, layout JSON `hd`, `nlOn crumb/newb/sort` | `NativeList.HEAD` / `ls_nlh='0'` |
| 10 | Top bar / selection bar, New-folder button, bottom dock + clipboard strip, snackbar, download cards | `NlChrome.kt`, bridge `nlChrome`, `nlChPush()` / `nlCh()` | `NlChrome.ENABLED` / `ls_nlc='0'` |
| 11 | Side drawer (tabs, rows, footer) painted natively; ui.html mirrors the DOM into flat draw lists (`nlDrFlat` / `nlDrBuild`) | `NlDrawer.kt`, `nlDr(...)` | `NlDrawer.ENABLED` / `ls_nld='0'` |

Other native pieces: `NlSearch.kt` (native twin of `#srow`, rect from `nlSrPush()`), `NlSheets.kt` (View pane / sort sheets, `St.gal`), `NlFileIcons.kt` (coloured Word / Excel / PowerPoint / PDF / zip / apk / image / video / audio icons), `NlSplash.kt` (instant start: on pause it stores the first 120 rows of the shown folder, palette, list geometry and two small pictures of the page chrome and draws them natively while the WebView still loads ui.html; drawing the last folder briefly before the Main menu is a known quirk), `UiScale` (text scale `UiScale.tf`).

**Look decisions (all native + page twin)**
- **Classic folder** (paper-sheet icon) in every view: `NlClassicFolder` (100 x 87.7 unit box) used by `NlGridView.folder` and `NlRowView`; page twin `FOLD` (viewBox 100x88); `#list.v-grid .fold svg` 100 % wide.
- **Grid cards**: no date line (count / size only), folder icon 62 % of the card square; the category badge (DCIM, Download, Music...) is drawn INSIDE the folder icon (centre of the front panel); with a badge the folder grows to >= 62 dp (max 94 % of the card) so a 24 dp badge fits.
- **Narrow rows** (split screen): second line (count left, date right) - date loses its time, then ellipsizes, then hides; compact view caps the right text at 45 %.
- **New folder button hid the last files**: `NativeList.ensureMgr` gives the RecyclerView 88 dp extra bottom padding (`clipToPadding = false`, list and grid); page fallback `#list::after` spacer 88 px + safe area.
- Row divider 2 px (CSS `.row` border-bottom + `NativeList` divider); top bar 52 px with 48 px buttons and 30 px icons (`.bar .ibtn`, `NlChrome` `NlBarView`); hamburger drawn 32 dp (touch target 40 dp); drawer "Main menu" row bigger (`.di.mm`: icon 44, title 23 px, subtitle 15 px, row 76 px).
- **Text size fix**: natively painted text (`NativeList`, `NlHead`, `NlDrawer`, `NlChrome`) multiplies `UiScale.tf` (set in `UiScale.wrap` / `save`), so Settings > Text size works on phones that show the native list. Row heights are fixed: text at 150-160 % may clip.
- **Previews in the list**: PDFs show their first page with a small "PDF" pill (`Thumbs.make` renders `.pdf` with `PdfRenderer`: page 1, 480 px wide on white, a tall page cut to its top square, JPEG 75, same disk cache, one at a time via `pdfLock`; password-protected / broken PDFs keep the icon; rows get a `path` for local PDFs 1 B - 60 MB in BOTH row builders; `/api/thumb` answers PDFs; not for other devices / SMB / archives). Office files: embedded preview image (8.5). Subfolder-search hits ("In subfolders (n)", `nlBuild` branch `i._h`) send `p`, `s`, `t` for pictures, videos and local PDFs so they get thumbnails; hits stay non-selectable and tapping one goes to its folder.
- **Pull to refresh**: file browser (native `SwipeRefreshLayout` / page `#ptr`); Gallery / Videos album home (`galForce()` zeroes `GALT` and re-reads `/api/albums`, because album lists are cached 15 s); inside an album (`pullBind(sc, box, go, ok)`, 70 px threshold, not while items are selected).
- **Select-all** toggles (second tap with everything selected unselects) in Gallery / Videos albums (`galView`), AI-search results (`isView`) and the file browser (`#allsel`).
- **Edge swipe** (`MainActivity.dispatchTouchEvent`): swipe right from anywhere below the header (edge 40 dp, rest of the screen 72 dp and mostly horizontal; starting in the header = path / device chips scroll) toggles the **Main menu** (`lsEdgeOk()` + header bottom reported to Kotlin every 200 ms via `LSAndroid.edgeOk(ok, top, vw)`; ui.html `lsEdgeOpen()` -> `homeShow()` / `homeHide()` + `nlLay(true)`); ignored when a dialog / sheet / viewer / selection / search is open. A 32 x 200 dp left-edge strip is excluded from the system Back gesture (Android 10+). The old "swipe right = previous device" is gone (swipe left = next device stays).

### 6.3 Main menu (home screen, "Start menu")
**Status**: Material 3 look implemented as a `#home` overlay in ui.html (not compiled in the notes; JS `node --check` only).
- **Design history**: first approved design = grid of big rounded icon tiles (design artifact `https://claude.ai/artifact/VsN5t5zJd7xkHJmvMj7kT7`, source once in the zip as `design/HomeMenu.dc.html`); replaced on 2026-10-10 by **Material 3**: storage card on top (live used / free from `/api/space`, fallback `ls_used`), then sections **Browse** (Gallery -> `/Pictures`, Videos -> `/Movies`, Downloads -> `/Download`) and **Network and tools** (Printers, SMB Shares, Storage Analyzer disabled at first; later replaced by **Favorite folders**, and a **Devices** tile was added - their exact section is not recorded). Tiles use theme tokens (`--sel --cont --ac`) + per-tile `--hc1..7`; dark theme has own `--hm*` vars; icon paths in `HM_P` (`HM_P.sto` kept for a Storage Analyzer tile that was removed 2026-10-11).
- **Tiles**: Gallery / Videos / Downloads show "size (files)" (`GET /api/folderstat`; last value from `localStorage` `ls_hs2_<path>` shown at once, refreshed when the phone has counted; cache key was renamed from `ls_hs_` so the old thumbnail-inflated number is not shown). **Network and tools row (since 2026-10-28)**: SMB Shares -> `openSmb()`, **FTP servers** / **Cloud servers** -> `srvPicker('FTP' | 'Drive')` (saved servers listed here only, not in Devices). The old Printers and Favorite folders tiles are gone (Printers: `pickPrinter(..., busy)` still reachable from the drawer). **Devices** (`HM_P.dev`, subtitle = number of other devices, `hmDevSub()`) -> `devPicker(true)` bottom sheet (This device + all peers, "Add by IP" row replaces the "+ IP" chip). The **Favorite folders** tile (cascading popup `hmFavMenu`) was removed 2026-10-28; Quick folders live in the drawer. Opening the menu triggers `/api/scan` + `pollPeers()`.
- **Behaviour**: shown only on a **fresh launch** (`MainActivity.showHome` = `b == null` and the intent has no SEND / VIEW / PRINT action, read by `Bridge.homeStart()`), never on a share intent. `openLoc` / `openDev` call `homeHide()`. `lsBack`: home is the bottom of the stack (Back there leaves the app); Back at the root of main storage returns to home. The three-dot button opens Settings (there is no overflow menu). The drawer's first row is "Main menu"; the drawer tab strip (Folders / Favorites / Recent) and the old Favorites / Recent-history features were removed 2026-10-11 (`#dtabs` stays in the DOM, `display:none`, `NlDrawer` hides its tab view). Pictures in Quick folders is no longer permanent (removable, not re-added at start; reset to default brings it back).
- Other related edits: the grey "x free of y" line above the root crumb is hidden (`#free{display:none}`); the devices chip bar `#peers` is hidden (`renderPeers` still builds it); the disk-percentage **pill** (`#used` / `NlHead` pressKind 4 -> `nlOn('used')`) now calls `pillTap()` = Storage Analyzer (12) with fallback `spaceInfo()` (usage bar + this folder's counts).
- **Checklist**: launch -> menu shows its tiles; each tile opens its screen and Back returns to the menu; share a file into the app -> send dialog (no menu); rotate / small text size / dark theme -> tiles fit (`UiScale`); the edge swipe toggles menu <-> folder.
- Open: "every start vs fresh launch only" unanswered; maybe a Settings switch "Start with: Home / last folder"; live subtitles, long-press to reorder / hide tiles, more tiles (Documents, Apps, New files, Recent, Remote, Trash like *File Manager +*).

### 6.4 Gallery (Pictures / Videos)
- Album cards are page-rendered (`.albums`, `mkAlbum` / `mkAlbumX`, `galFetch`). A card opens **`galView(a)`** (`#gal` overlay): that album's pictures + videos as one square-thumbnail grid (newest first, chunked); Back closes (`lsBack`, `o._sel`). `GCX` (+ `cxd / cxp / cxl`) makes `nativeImg`, `nativePlay`, `mediaList`, `openMedia` read the album instead of `S.dev/S.path/shown()`. `#gal` hides the native chrome like `#pv`.
- **Hold menu**: press-and-hold selects like the file browser (selection `GS` local to the album); bottom bar `#gal .gb`: Copy, **Move** (`galPick(a)` sheet lists the other albums of the same kind + "New album" -> clip(cut) + `/api/paste`), Share (any number of files; `LSAndroid.shareFiles({items:[{name,url,size}]})` -> `MainActivity.shareFilesImpl` fetches into `cache/open` with one cancellable progress notification, then `shareFileList` opens the chooser via FileProvider), Print (`doPrint(preset, cx)` takes an explicit `{items,path,done}` context; hidden for video albums), Details (`doDetails(it, pth)`), Delete (-> Recycle bin). Selected thumbnails shrink into a rounded tile with an accent check; rounded floating bar, slide animations (CSS `#gal .gb`, `.gh.sl`, `.gc.sel`).
- **Copy/Move if new (2026-10-22)**: bar button **Copy/Move new** -> Move or Copy -> pick album -> `Jobs` conflict mode `unsim`: every file is compared with the pictures already in the target folder (`DupFinder.SimIndex.match`: same shape + difference hash <= 20 of 256 bits = same picture in another name / size / quality; videos and undecodable files only when byte-identical). Match = skipped, original stays (also for Move); no match + same name = renamed `name (1)`; new folders are renamed in one step on the same volume, otherwise copy + delete. Result note: `N moved/copied, S skipped`. Moving a folder: files that were copied are removed from the source one by one, skipped look-alikes stay (the folder is removed only when nothing was skipped). Same check in the paste conflict card (**Only if not similar**) and in **Merge > Skip look-alikes**. Limits: first run in a big target decodes every target picture once (slow, cached per job); videos are not compared visually. **Test**: album A has `IMG_1.jpg`, album B has a resized copy named `x.jpg` + a different `IMG_1.jpg`: Move A->B skips the pair, renames the other; cancel mid-way; Copy keeps originals; Merge with Skip look-alikes leaves look-alikes in their album.
- **Per-file ask card: Only if not similar (2026-10-22)**: the "Already exists - replace?" card (copy / move / paste / merge, this phone only) has a 5th button; answer `unsim` / `unsim_all` ("all remaining" = sticky): look-alike (`DupFinder.look`) -> skipped, different picture -> kept as `name (1)`. `Jobs.work` + `AlbumMerge.work`; UI `lvAsk` button list in `ui.html`.
- **Small albums hidden** by default (<= 1 item, Android resource folders `mipmap-*`, `drawable-*`; `galJunk`, key `ls_galhide`; button under the albums toggles).
- **View pane in the Gallery**: only **Albums: 2 / 3 / 4 columns** (`NlSheets.kt` `St.gal`; `S.cols`; saved with view / sort in `ls_g` / `ls_pf` so "Apply to all folders" works; the page also sends `gc: S.cols` in the layout JSON). Album covers are 4:3 (`.alb .acv`, `object-fit: cover`, centred 50 % / 35 %); thumbnails themselves keep the aspect ratio.
- **AI search entries**: press and hold an album of /Pictures -> bottom-bar **AI search** (`albHold`; hidden for video albums) -> `isView(sdir, sname)` limited to that folder; albums that are NOT direct folders of /Pictures (DCIM/Camera, WhatsApp..., `mkAlbumX`) open a popup "Search the pictures in this album with AI?".
- **Hide** (2026-10-11): hold a file / folder on this phone -> **Hide** (not in the Gallery album home, which has its own). Hidden paths share the list of hidden albums (`HGAL` / `ls_galhid`, saved by `hgSave()`); **Settings > Gallery settings > Hidden albums, files and folders** lists them with Unhide / Unhide all; filtered like dot-files (`vis()`, search hits via `hidP` / `hidAnc`), come back with **Show hidden files**; native side via bridge `nlHidden(json)` -> `NativeList.setHidden` / `NlModel.hidden`. Files are not renamed. Side effect: albums hidden before are now also hidden inside plain folders. This phone only.

### 6.4a Other phones' gallery (read-only, 2026-10-23; merged from `HANDOVER_GALLERY.md`)
**Goal.** On the local Gallery / Videos main page, *Other phones* lists the other LANShare phones; opening one shows THAT phone's gallery like the local one (album cards, covers, counts, grid, viewers). View, open, copy to this phone, AI search and print; no move / hide / delete / merge. Not run on a device (JS checked with `node --check`; Kotlin built by CI only).
- **Backend (`core/Routes.kt`)**: peer routes `/p/albums?kind=vid|img` (`Albums.json` of that phone), `/p/thumb?path=&big=1`, `/p/vthumb?path=` (video JPEG + `X-Duration`), `/p/imgsearch?mode=status|visual|text&q=&whole=&dir=` (search runs ON the remote phone with its own index and model). Local UI routes `/api/ralbums?dev=[&kind=vid]` (404 text "Update LANShare on <name>" for an old build), `/api/rthumb?dev=&path=[&big=1][&video=1]` (falls back to `RemoteThumbs.make` for old builds), `/api/rimgs?dev=&mode=...` (forwards, 90 s timeout), helper `remotePhone(dev)`. Album / cover paths are relative to that phone's storage root, the same virtual paths `/api/ls`, `/api/dl`, `/api/file?dev=<id>` use. Both phones need the new build for fast thumbs and albums.
- **Albums UI (`ui.html`)**: `galRoot()` is device independent (`/@gallery`, `/@videos` on ANY dev); `galFetch()` caches per kind AND dev (`GALPK='img|<dev>'`, `GALA.dev`, `GALE`); `mkAlbumR(a)` = remote album card (read-only); `galPhoneBar()` chip *Other phones* / *<phone name>* -> `galPhones()` sheet (This phone + online / offline `kind==='LANShare'` peers); remote also gets a Photos / Videos chip. Hidden-albums (`ls_galhid`) do not apply to remote albums; *Hide small albums* is shared.
- **Inside a remote album**: `galView(a)` takes `a.dev` (`D`, `rem`); list = `/api/ls?dev=D`, `GCX.dev=D` so image / video viewers stream `/api/dl?dev=D`; thumbs via `/api/rthumb` (no duration badge on remote videos). Selection bar: *To this phone* (album picker -> clip copy from D -> `/api/paste` local; `galForceLocal()` drops the album cache afterwards), Copy, Share, Details, **Print**.
- **AI search**: `risView(D,dn,sdir,sname)` in the `#gal` overlay: Visual / Text in pictures tabs, Whole word / Contains, status card (that phone has no model / no scan yet, old build -> its 404). Entry: chip *Search* in `galPhoneBar()` (remote Photos home = whole phone) and the search icon in a remote album header. Scanning / model download is done on the other phone; videos are not searched.
- **Print**: button in the remote selection bar (photos only) and in `risView` results -> `doPrint(null,{items,dev:D,...})`; no backend change (`Jobs.startPrint` / `PdfPrint` / `WifiPrint` read files through `Jobs.ep(srcId)` = `RemoteFs`); history `phAdd` stores `dev:D` so re-print works.
- **Open ideas**: duration badge for remote videos, per-dev hidden albums. Device check: albums list, album grid, viewer for photo and video, copy to this phone, search (visual and text), print (this phone, Wi-Fi, PC, PDF, sheet layout).

- **PC pictures gallery (2026-10-27, NOT compiled / run)**: each saved SMB share (a PC) is listed in the *Other phones* sheet under "PC". Opening it shows ONLY the PC's Pictures folder and its sub-folders as album cards (photos only: no Videos chip, no AI search). `core/PcPictures.kt`: `find()` looks for Pictures / Resimler / Bilder ... (a share that is the folder, `<share>/Pictures`, `<share>/Users/<name>/Pictures`; Public / Default skipped; newest wins), `albums()` walks it with `lsFast` (2500 folders / 100 s, cached 90 s; one album per folder that holds pictures; junk folders skipped), `setDir()` stores `picdir` in the share (kept by `Smb.update`). Routes: `/api/ralbums?dev=smb:..[&fresh=1]` (smb branch), `GET/POST /api/pcpicdir`. Thumbs = existing `/api/rthumb` fallback (`RemoteThumbs.make`, whole picture pulled once, cached). UI: `isPc()`, `galPhones()` PC rows, `galPhoneBar()` chip *Pictures folder* -> `pcPicDir()` (type a path, empty = automatic).

### 6.5 View options (View pane)
List view: **thumbnails small / medium / large** (`thumb: s|m|l`; medium row 72, lead 62x56), grid view: **columns 2 / 3 / 4** (`cols: 0|2|3|4`, none = automatic: 2 on phones, 4 from 600 dp), **Folders first** on / off (`ff: 1|0`; off = folders sorted with files, size order treats folders as 0 B). Stored next to view / sort in `ls_g` / `ls_pf` (so "Apply to all folders" covers them). Code: ui.html (`applyPrefs`, `setPref`, `shown`, `openView`, `nlSheetState`, `nlPref`, `nlCfg` 7 fields `...,thumb,ff`, `nlLayout` `md` + `gc`, CSS `.t-md`, `.gc2-4`), `NlSheets.kt`, `NlModel.kt` (`Cfg.ff`), `NativeList.kt` (`NlRowView.medium`, `gcols`, `instantPref`). History: a missing `gc` / `md` key in the layout JSON used to reset a chosen 4-column grid to 2 on every layout push (fixed: a missing key keeps the current value). The notes of 2026-10-10 disagree on whether the page side persists `cols` / `thumb m` / `ff` - **verify in ui.html**.

### 6.6 Folder colours and icons (Style, 2026-10-12)
Select folders -> bottom bar **Style** (palette icon; not inside archives) -> sheet with live preview, 11 colours + default, 20 icons + none, Reset / Done. Stored in the page as `FSTY` (`ls_fsty`, key `dev|path`, value `{c:'#rrggbb', i:iconKey}`), so it works for this phone, SMB and other devices; the native list only gets this phone's entries through bridge `nlFStyle(json)` (`{"/path":[colourARGB, icon, iconColourARGB]}` -> `NativeList.setFStyle` -> `NlModel.fstyle`). Drawing: `NlClassicFolder.draw(..., tint)` (front panel = colour, back tab x0.88, outline x0.78; sheets stay white; `foldSvg()` twin), `NlRow.fcol` (page key `fc`, in `sig`). The chosen icon replaces the category badge, coloured with a dark shade of the folder colour. 13 Material icons added to `NlIcons.raw` and `IC` (star heart home work school games lock cloud build code bookmark place flight) + `palette`. Not covered: Gallery album covers, folder pickers, album cards. Style follows the folder PATH (rename / move drops it).

### 6.7 Fonts and text size
45 Google Fonts (OFL, Latin + Latin-Ext / Turkish subset, `assets/fonts/ff_<id>.ttf`, ~4.9 MB) + System. `Fnt.LIST` / `Fnt.reload`, `UiScale.font` / `saveFont`, bridge `fontGet` / `fontList` / `uiSet2`, `/appfont/<id>.ttf` served in `shouldInterceptRequest`, font picker with preview in Interface settings, a head script applies it to the page. Unreferenced fonts (`tnr-regular`, `tnr-bold`, `urbanist-medium`, `urbanist-semibold`, 196 KB) are listed in 15.2.

### 6.8 Quick references (page functions used from several places)
`openLoc(dev, path)`, `openDev(id)`, `lsGoLocal(p)` (= `openLoc('local', p)`, used by "Show in file manager"), `pickPrinter(here, wifi, peers)`, `showPrinters()`, `openSmb()`, `devPicker(all)`, `drawerOpen/Close/Render`, `pillTap()`, `spaceInfo()`, `isView()`, `galView()`, `openPrintQueue(pc)`, `openPcScreen(pc)`, `peOpenOnPc()`, `nativeOffice(i)`, `doNewOffice`.
