## 7. Viewers and editors (video, image, PDF, text)

All are plain `Activity` classes (no AppCompat, no Compose), hand-built views, `JobOverlay.install(this)` after `setContentView`. They only accept `http://127.0.0.1` / `localhost` URLs (the local server's `/api/dl`, which does Range requests, so local / peer / SMB files stream without downloading). Handover of a list to a viewer goes through a static `pending` object (no Binder size limit). **Not compiled / not device-tested in the notes** (see section 2).

### 7.1 Video player (`PlayerActivity.kt`, `PlayerWidgets.kt`)
- Media3 / ExoPlayer 1.3.1 (`media3-exoplayer`, `media3-ui`). Tapping a video calls `LSAndroid.play(json)` (`MainActivity.Bridge.play`) with the folder's videos as a playlist: `{start, items:[{name, url, key, subs:[{name, url}]}]}`. Browsers (no `LSAndroid.play`) keep the HTML5 viewer (`openMedia`); inside it a play button / the error box offer "Play in player".
- MKV / AVI / MOV / WebM / MP4 / FLV / MPEG, audio + subtitle track menus, speed, next / previous, resume position (SharedPreferences `ls_player`, key `dev|path|size`), **PiP** (Home while playing, or the button), double-tap +-10 s, swipe left = brightness / right = volume, Fit / Zoom / Stretch, rotate, auto landscape for wide videos.
- Sidecar subtitles (same base name, `.srt .vtt .ass .ssa`) fetched, converted to UTF-8 (windows-1254 fallback) into `cache/subs` and attached.
- Manifest: `PlayerActivity` is its own task (`singleTask`, empty affinity) so PiP works; theme `Theme.LANShare.Player`. No background-audio service (audio stops when the screen is off / PiP closed). `PlayerWidgets.kt`: colours (light = white page, dark = black) and canvas-drawn icons (no drawable resources or font glyphs).

### 7.2 Image viewer (`ImageViewerActivity.kt`, `androidx.viewpager2`)
- `LSAndroid.viewImages(json)` = `{start, items:[{name, url, size}]}` in the current sort order. Each picture is fetched from `/api/dl` into `cache/img/<n>.bin`, decoded with `inSampleSize` (longest side <= 3072 / 4096 px by memory class, never above the 4096 GPU limit), EXIF-rotated, kept in an LruCache with the neighbours preloaded. `ZoomImageView` (`ScaleType.MATRIX`): pinch, double-tap (fit <-> 2.5x), pan, at the edge of a zoomed picture the drag becomes a page swipe. Tap hides the bars. `.gif` / `.svg` (and `.heic` below Android 9, `LSAndroid.sdk()`) go to the HTML viewer. Originals above ~4096 px are not shown at full pixel resolution.
- **Top bar redesign (2026-10-10)**: two lines - middle-ellipsized file name + `date · time`; position in a pill at the bottom centre (`countPill`); drawn Material icon buttons (`IconBtn`, 44 dp): text layer (T), edit, share, and a **⋮ menu** (Slideshow / Stop slideshow, **Details**: name, modified, pixels, size, position). Slideshow 4 s, stops on manual swipe.
- Text layer / OCR: see 11.2. Viewer also reads `OcrPrefs` and has `AutoOcr`.

### 7.3 Image edit (`ImageEditActivity.kt`, 2026-10-08)
Pencil button in the viewer: rotate left / right, drag-to-crop, resize by px or 100 / 75 / 50 / 25 %. Preview is a 2048 px bitmap; on Save the operations are applied once to the original at full resolution (`ImgEdit.render`: EXIF orientation -> rotation -> crop -> scale, one Canvas pass). Save as copy (`name (edited).ext` next to the original; for peers / SMB / archive entries into `Pictures/LANShare Edited`) or replace the original (this phone, jpg / png / webp only); the viewer reloads the page after a replace and drops the page's OCR cache.

### 7.4 PDF viewer (`PdfViewerActivity.kt`)
- Built on `PdfRenderer` (so: no document search, no in-page selection, no links, and **no streaming** - it needs the whole file).
- **Viewer 1 (2026-10-10)**: fast scroller (`PdfScrollBar`, right edge, bubble `page / total`); page **overview grid** (▦, 3 columns, 5 from 600 dp; thumbnails 260 px on the `pdf-render` thread, 12 MB cache); **page text** (T): renders the current page up to 2400 px and reads it with OCR into a selectable dialog with Copy / Share (works for scans; OCR, not the PDF's own text).
- **Viewer 2 (2026-10-10)**: after scrolling stops the next two and previous page are pre-rendered (`prefetch`, `ahead` set; a page with no picture first gets a quick 1/3-size render); in the overview, long-press selects pages (tap toggles, All / clear), bottom bar **Print** (all pages when none selected), **Save PDF** (to Downloads, `name (p. 3-5).pdf`, MediaStore on API 29+), **Share**. Print uses the system print dialog (`PrintDocumentAdapter`): the whole document goes as the original file; selected pages / a range are rebuilt by `JpegPdfWriter` (one JPEG per page at ~180 dpi, one bitmap in memory; text in such copies is not searchable). **Big files**: from other devices / SMB, kept in `cache/pdfc` for 12 h (400 MB cap, oldest first; same key + size = same file, so a same-size edit shows stale up to 12 h), interrupted downloads resume with a Range request (3 tries); local files read fresh.

### 7.5 Text editor (`TextEditorActivity.kt`, `CodeHl.kt`, `MdRender.kt`)
**No handover existed for these.** What the code headers say: `CodeHl` = small dependency-free syntax highlighter (`scan` returns packed triples start / end / kind); `MdRender` = light Markdown -> blocks for the editor's preview (headings with slugs / outline, bold / italic / strike, `code`, nested bullet / numbered / task lists, nested quotes, rules, links incl. `#anchor` and reference links, front matter, simple HTML; no library, no WebView). Images are only tappable labels (no loading); relative links to other files are not opened. The old docx branch of `TextEditorActivity` (`DOCX_MAX`, read-only banner) was meant to be removed when Word files moved to the Office viewer (`docx` is no longer in `ED_MORE`); `core/DocxText.kt` is still in the repo and unused (15.2). `ED_MORE` (ui.html) is the editable-extension list.

---

## 8. Office files (Word / Excel / PowerPoint)

### 8.1 Summary and design decisions
Replaces the old read-only "docx -> Markdown" viewer with proper support: view `.docx .xlsx` (+ `.xlsm .xltx`), `.xls`, `.doc`, `.odt`, `.ods`, `.pptx`, `.odp`; edit text / cells in place without destroying the rest of the file (docx, xlsx); create new files; search, print / PDF, CSV export, thumbnails, launcher shortcuts.
Decisions to keep:
- **No new Gradle dependencies** (no Apache POI: too big / slow, needs API 26+ parts). Own streaming parsers.
- Package `com.lanshare.app.core.office`: pure Kotlin + `java.util.zip`, **no `android.*` imports**, so it runs on a plain JVM. Own pull parser `MiniXml` (local names only; `rawAttr("r:id")` when needed).
- Files open from a **local `File`** (callers copy remote / SAF / SMB files into cache first).
- **Edit strategy**: patch the XML in place and re-zip, copying every other entry byte-for-byte (styles, images, charts, macros survive). Never regenerate a workbook / document from the model.
- Limits protect the phone: 64 MB per part, 5000 rows x 200 cols / 250k cells per sheet (`XSheet.truncated`).

### 8.2 Self-test (JVM) and fixtures
```
kotlinc app/src/main/java/com/lanshare/app/core/office/*.kt tools/office_selftest/Main.kt -include-runtime -d /tmp/ost.jar
java -jar /tmp/ost.jar tools/office_selftest/sample.xlsx          # also .docx .xls .doc .odt .ods .pptx .odp
java -jar /tmp/ost.jar --edit | --docx | --export <file>
```
kotlinc 2.0.21 (from `github.com/JetBrains/kotlin/releases`) works in the sandbox. Fixtures in `tools/office_selftest/`: `sample.xlsx / .xls / .ods / .docx / .doc / .odt / .pptx / .odp`, `sample_pics.pptx` (pictures + notes), `sample_table.pptx` (table + hidden slide). Expected output per fixture is in the section 8.3 rows. Known: `sample_table.pptx` prints `FAIL content search` by design (no "welcome" in it).
Open bug from Part 6b: `--docx` printed `FAIL replace split runs`; Part 9 found it was a **test-order bug** (the self-test overwrote the paragraph before searching it); the test was moved, `DocxEditor` unchanged.

### 8.3 Core modules (all in `core/office/`)
| Module | Role | Notes / gaps |
|---|---|---|
| `MiniXml` | pull parser (`next()` START / END / TEXT / EOF, `attr`, `rawAttr`, `readText`, `skip`, `isEmptyElement`); entity + `_xHHHH_` unescape; UTF-8 / 16 BOM; CDATA, comments, PIs, DOCTYPE | |
| `OoxmlPackage` | `ZipFile`-backed, case-insensitive part lookup, size cap, `rels(part)` with target resolution, `read`, `xml` | KDoc must not contain `/*` |
| `NumFmt` | number formats ids 0-49 + custom (General, 0 / 0.00 / #,##0, %, E+00, literals, `[$USD]`, 2-3 sections, dates / times, 1900 (fake 02-29) and 1904 systems, `isDate()`) | conditional sections `[>100]` not evaluated; fractions approximate; English names / separators only |
| `XlsxModel`, `XlsxReader` | `XCell(row, col, text, raw, kind, formula, style)`, `XSheet` (sparse rows, merges, widths, hidden rows / cols, frozen panes, `truncated`), `XStyles`, `XRef`; `XlsxReader(file).use { sheets; readSheet(i, maxRows, maxCols, maxCells) }` | |
| `DocxModel`, `DocxReader` | `DDoc(body, header, footer, footnotes, endnotes, defaultSize, truncated)`; blocks `DPara`, `DTable`; `DRun` (TEXT / TAB / BREAK / PAGE / IMAGE / NOTE, formatting, link, `DImage`); style chain + docDefaults, numbering labels, hyperlinks, `mc:AlternateContent` Choice only, `w:sdt` unwrapped, text boxes as extra paragraphs, `ins` kept / `del` dropped, first section's default header / footer | gaps: numbering overrides, theme colours, first-page / even headers, complex fields (cached result only), tab stops, spacing |
| `DocxHtml`, `XlsxHtml` | HTML renderers (`DocxHtml.render(doc, image, title)`; `XlsxHtml.render(sheet, tabs, active, styles)` with sticky column letters / row numbers, frozen panes, merges, widths, hidden rows / cols, text styles, fill, align, wrap, tab bar `lsoffice://sheet/N`) | theme colours unresolved (plain `#RRGGBB` only) |
| `XlsxEditor` | each call = load -> patch -> temp file -> sanity open -> swap, `.bak~` kept until the swap worked: `setCells`, `setCell`, `insertRows`, `deleteRows`, `renameSheet`, `addSheet`, `deleteSheet` (0-based) | typing: `=` formula, number (<= 15 chars, `007` stays text), true / false bool, else text; every edit drops `calcChain.xml` and sets `fullCalcOnLoad`; `deleteSheet` refuses the last visible sheet. **Not adjusted**: formulas, defined names, conditional formatting / validation ranges, hyperlinks, drawings, table parts |
| `XlsxWriter` | `createEmpty`, `fromCsv(out, csv, sheetName, delimiter?, headerBold)` (auto delimiter, quotes, BOM), `write`, `parseCsv`; header row frozen when > 1 row | dates stay text |
| `DocxEditor` | same patch-in-place pattern: `paragraphCount`, `paragraphText`, `setParagraphText` (keeps pPr + first run's rPr), `insertParagraph`, `deleteParagraph`, `cellText` / `setCellText`, `replaceAll(file, find, replace, matchCase, wholeWord)` (body, headers, footers, notes; across split runs) | **Undo**: old file kept as `<name>.undo1~` .. `.undo10~` (`undo`, `undoDepth`, `clearUndo`). Refused: paragraphs with images, drawings, fields. Replace drops hyperlinks / bookmarks in the edited paragraph; no tracked changes |
| `DocxWriter` | `createEmpty`, `fromText`, `fromMarkdown` (headings, lists, quote, fences, rule, pipe tables, inline styles, links); A4, Calibri 11 | no images |
| `OleFile`, `XlsReader` | OLE2 / CFB reader; `.xls` BIFF8 into the same `XSheet` / `XStyles` (SST + CONTINUE, LABEL / NUMBER / RK / MULRK / FORMULA cached value, formats, XF, palette, merges, col info, hidden rows, frozen panes) | formula text not decoded, BIFF5 throws, FILEPASS = "File is password protected", no rich text / comments |
| `DocReader` | `.doc` (FIB, piece table, paragraphs, cell marks, fields, `HYPERLINK`, tables from PAPX) into `DDoc`; read-only | not read: character formatting, styles, images, headers / footers, footnotes, column widths |
| `OdfReader` | `.odt` -> `DDoc` (styles, lists, links, images, boxes, notes, tables with spans); `.ods` -> `XSheet` (`of:=SUM([.A1:.A3])` -> `=SUM(A1:A3)`, repeats, merges, hidden, frozen from settings.xml - unverified) | encrypted = password message |
| `PptxReader`, `OdpReader` | `.pptx`: slides in `presentation.xml` order, "Slide N" headings, title placeholders as sub-headings, pictures, speaker notes ("Notes: ..." italic grey), tables (spans, `firstRow` header), hidden slides "(hidden)"; `.odp`: "Slide N - <name>", title frames, `draw:image`, notes | old binary `.ppt` unsupported ("save as .pptx"); no shapes / layout / animations |
| `OfficeOpen` | dispatcher by content (zip: `mimetype` / `xl/workbook.xml` / `word/document.xml` / pptx; OLE: `Workbook` -> xls, `WordDocument` -> doc, `EncryptedPackage` -> password message); API `format`, `isSheet`, `editable`, `sheets`, `readSheet(i)`, `readDoc()`, `image(part)` | |
| `OfficeExport` | `sheetToCsv` (RFC 4180, UTF-8 BOM, CRLF, hidden rows / cols skipped), `csvName`, `text(file)` (400k char cap), `textOrNull`, `containsOrFalse(file, q)` (false for encrypted / corrupt / > 30 MB), `isOffice(name)`, `EXTS` | |
| `OfficeThumb` | embedded preview image (`docProps/thumbnail.*`, `Thumbnails/thumbnail.png`) | files made by this app and `.doc` / `.xls` carry none |
Self-test sample files are in `tools/office_selftest/`; their expected outputs were dropped from this file - run the self-test to see them.
### 8.4 Android integration (not compiled in the notes)
- **`OfficeViewerActivity`** (package `com.lanshare.app`, plain `Activity`, `Theme.LANShare` - needs an ActionBar for the Find menu): extra `path` = local file (else it copies `intent.data` / `EXTRA_STREAM` to cache); docx via `DocxHtml` with images inlined as data: URIs (< 3 MB each), sheets via `XlsxHtml` (+ styles); JS disabled, file access off, only `lsoffice://` navigation; find-in-page SearchView, pinch zoom; password-protected message. Menu: **Print / save as PDF** (`WebView.createPrintDocumentAdapter`), **Export sheet as CSV** (`ACTION_CREATE_DOCUMENT`, request code 77), **Edit** (docx / xlsx opened from the file list, `dev` + `rpath` extras): xlsx "Edit cell..." / "Insert row...", docx "Find and replace..." / "Add paragraph..."; each edit runs on the cache copy, is uploaded back to the source at once (`Jobs.ep(dev).write`), then the viewer reloads. **Undo last edit** (10a): `mutate` copies the cache file to `cache/office-undo/<name>.<nanos>.u~` before each edit (max 10), undo restores + uploads; snapshots and `DocxEditor.clearUndo` are cleaned in `onDestroy`. Recent files are recorded only when opened from the file list.
- **Hand-off**: ui.html ext kind `office` (doc docx odt xls xlsx ods + pptx odp) -> `nativeOffice(i)` -> `LSAndroid.viewOffice` -> `MainActivity.viewOffice(json)` downloads to `cache/office/` (60 MB cap, files > 1 h purged) and starts the viewer. Fallback in browsers: `/api/office?dev=local&path=..&sheet=N` (`OfficeWeb.kt`: same renderers, images inlined, sandbox CSP, <= 60 MB, local only) opens in a new tab. Manifest: `exported=true` VIEW + SEND filters for the office MIME types (incl. pptx / odp).
- **Exact pages (Office fidelity Part 2, 2026-10-24)**: opening an office file first asks the paired PC to convert it to PDF (`OfficeExact`, `Jobs.converterIp` + `officeToPdf`) and shows it in `PdfViewerActivity`; simple HTML view is the fallback and the "Aa" button. See `HANDOVER_OFFICE_FIDELITY.md` Part 2 notes.
- **New files**: + menu "Word document" / "Excel workbook" -> `doNewOffice` -> `LSAndroid.newOffice({dev, dir, kind})` -> `MainActivity.newOffice` (free name "New document (2).docx", `DocxWriter / XlsxWriter.createEmpty`, written through `Jobs.ep(dev).write`, then `lsRefresh()`).
- **Content search**: `/api/search?...&content=1` -> `LocalFs.searchOfficeContent` (local only, q >= 2 chars, 100 results, 20 s, skips > 30 MB); "In files" toggle `#scont` / `S.cont` in the search row.
- **Thumbnails**: `/api/thumb` serves the embedded preview (404 when absent); `thMake` kind `office` in the HTML list; native list (10a): zip-based office files 1 B - 60 MB on this phone get a path in the row (`NlModel.rows` `OFFICE_TH`, ui.html `nlOffTh`, both `nlBuild` branches) and `NativeList.loadTh` branch `k == "office"` reads `OfficeThumb.embedded` directly. **Kind twin fix**: `NlModel.EXT` mapped docx / xls / ... to `doc` while ui.html maps them to `office` (`doc` = txt md rtf csv ppt only), giving different `sig`s; both now `office`, `NlIcons.path("office")` falls back to the `doc` icon.
- **Launcher shortcuts** (10d): `OfficeShortcutActivity` (invisible, `Theme.LANShare.Clear`, not exported): `ACT_NEW` (+ `kind`) creates an empty docx / xlsx with a free name in `/Documents` (`NEW_DIR`) and opens it; `ACT_OPEN` (+ `dev`, `rpath`) copies the file to `cache/office` (60 MB cap) and opens it; waits up to 6 s for `Core.local`; nested `Recent` (prefs `office_recent`, newest first, max 3). `Shortcuts.sync`: devices use `cap - 2` slots (max 6), then `office:new:docx`, `office:new:xlsx` (ranks 50 / 51), then `office:recent:N` (60+). Launchers show ~4 entries, so with many online devices the office ones may be cut.

### 8.4b Reading mode (2026-10-24)
Word-type documents (docx doc odt pptx odp) get a phone reading layout: menu **More > Reading mode** toggles it (default off, kept in prefs `office_read`: `on`, `px` 12-32 step 2, `theme` 0 paper / 1 sepia / 2 dark; default theme follows the app palette). When on, a row under the top bar shows **A-**, size, **A+**, and a colour button. `DocxHtml.render(doc, image, title, rd: DocxReading?)`: `rd == null` = old page layout (OfficeWeb and the self-test are unchanged). Reading layout: text reflows to the screen, own font size and line height, justify -> left, indents halved and capped, header / footer / page breaks hidden, tables scroll sideways in their own box, dark theme ignores author colours, highlights and cell fills. The viewer keeps the parsed `DDoc` (`curDoc`) so size / theme changes re-render without re-parsing and keep the reading position (scroll fraction restored in `onPageFinished`). Compiled and self-tested on a JVM (core only); the Android side (`OfficeViewerActivity`) is NOT compiled or run on a device. Device check: toggle on / off, A+ / A- keep the position, dark / sepia / paper, wide table scrolls, pinch zoom still works, find still works.

### 8.5 Known limits
Editing only for docx / xlsx opened from the file list; undo only within the current viewer session; doc / xls / odt / ods / pptx / odp read-only; xlsx theme colours, shared formulas (followers show value only), formulas not adjusted after row insert / delete; docx theme colours unresolved; `.doc` has no character formatting; `.ppt` unsupported; remote / SMB rows get no office thumbnails and no content search; big files: search skips > 30 MB, `/api/office` refuses > 60 MB; exact pages need a PC running `pcprint.py` v11+ (Settings > Office view; see 8.9), `phone_explorer.py` has no office preview (the PC web UI uses `/api/office`; `.odp` there untested). More limits per part are in 8.9.

### 8.6 First build: where the compiler will complain
`Routes.kt` new `search` / `office` branches, `OfficeWeb` (`android.util.Base64`), `XlsxHtml` local funs `sticky` / `look`, undo code in `OfficeViewerActivity` (`removeLastOrNull`, snapshot thread), `NlModel` / `NativeList` office thumbnail branches, `XlsxWriter.parseCsv` (`when` with `d ->`), `Regex.replace` lambdas with `return@replace` in `shiftMerges`, `Doc.use {}` on a private `AutoCloseable` in `DocxEditor`, `continue` inside `when` inside `while` in `XlsReader`.
Device check: office rows show thumbnails only for files that carry one (Word / Excel / LibreOffice saves do); "Undo last edit" appears after the first edit and disappears when the stack is empty; New Word doc creates `/Documents/New document.docx` and opens it; a recent shortcut reopens the same file.

### 8.9 Office fidelity expansion (Parts 1-13, 2026-10-24) - merged from `HANDOVER_OFFICE_FIDELITY.md` (file deleted in Part 13)

**Pipeline today.** Opening a Word / Excel / PowerPoint file: `OfficeViewerActivity.onCreate` -> unless the intent has `simple=true` or Settings > **Office view** is `Simple`, the file is sent to the paired PC (`pcprint.py` v11 `/convert`, Word or LibreOffice) -> PDF cached by content md5 (`core/OfficeExact.kt`, 12 h, 300 MB) -> native `PdfViewerActivity` (Part 2). On any failure (or `Simple`) the offline HTML view opens: bundled metric-compatible fonts (Part 3), Word page model + **Print layout** (Parts 4-5), floating pictures / text boxes / shapes, tables, tabs, theme (6-7), comments / tracked changes / links (8), Excel theme colours, conditional formatting, notes, widths (9), charts (10-11: bar / line / pie / doughnut / area / scatter), sheet pictures and shapes (11), Word outline, sheet list, formula bar, page strip (12).

**Setting (Part 13).** Settings > **Office view** (`ui.html` `openOfficeViewSettings`, JS bridge `LSAndroid.officeViewGet()` / `officeViewSet(mode)` in `MainActivity`, value stored by `OfficeExact.setMode`: SharedPreferences `office_view` / `mode` = `auto` | `exact` | `simple`). `auto` and `exact` behave the same today (the PC is tried first, the reason is toasted on failure); `simple` never contacts the PC. NOT compiled, NOT device-tested.

**Status of verification (honest).** Core (`core/office/*.kt` + `tools/office_selftest/Main.kt`) compiles with kotlinc 2.0.21 and ALL self-tests (Parts 3-12) pass with 0 FAIL in CI. **Parts 11 and 12 (`--chart2`, `--nav`) first ran on the JVM in CI on 2026-10-24 and passed** (earlier sandboxes had no kotlinc / network). **Android build: GitHub Actions run #513 (commit `66f731d`, 2026-10-24) built the APK successfully with Parts 11, 12 and 13 in it** (checked on github.com by Claude in the Part 13b session; job logs not readable). Nothing is device-tested. Part 13b added a second CI job `office-selftest` (`.github/workflows/build-apk.yml`): it compiles `core/office/*.kt` + `Main.kt` with kotlinc 2.0.21 and runs every self-test; a line starting `FAIL` or a crash turns the job red and the log is the artifact `office-selftest-log`. It does not block the APK build. **First result (run #514, 2026-10-24): green** - core compiled with kotlinc 2.0.21, all 13 modes ran with no `FAIL` line (checked from the log tail only: each mode finished in 0.1-0.2 s, so Part 13c made the job stricter: per mode it counts `ok` / `FAIL` lines against a minimum number of `ok` lines, goes red on a crash, a `FAIL` or too few `ok`, and writes a table to the run summary). **Run #515 (strict version) failed in 0 s with no test run at all: a bug in the CI script, not in the code** (`grep -c` exits 1 on a count of 0 and the step runs under `bash -e`). Fixed in Part 13d (`|| true`, `java < /dev/null`, the loop was simulated locally with a fake java: PASS / FAIL / crash / too-few-ok all reported and every mode still runs). **First REAL result of the strict job (run after Part 13d, 2026-10-24): all 13 modes PASS, 0 FAIL** - ok counts: default 24, `--docx` 16, `--edit` 14, `--fonts` 62, `--sections` 43, `--printtest` 11, `--float` 19, `--styles` 44, `--review` 24, `--cf` 53, `--chart` 44, `--chart2` 33 (first ever run), `--nav` 8 (first ever run). The same run's APK build was green (`BUILD SUCCESSFUL in 2m 28s`). Minimums in `build-apk.yml` can be raised to about 80% of these counts. What is still NOT verified: nothing of Parts 1-13 is device-tested, and the Android-only code (`OfficeViewerActivity`, `OfficeExact`, the Settings row, `ui.html` JS, the PC `/convert` route) has only been compiled, not run.

Fix what the compiler finds (Kotlin 1.9.24 on the Android build).
