## 9. Printing and the PC print service

### 9.1 Overview
Three ways to print, all from the same print dialog in `ui.html` (`doPrint`, `printOptions` for PC, `wifiOptions` for Wi-Fi printers):
- **PC** (`pcprint.py`, Windows service on port **8799**, LAN / Tailscale clients only): the phone converts what it can, sends the file with option query parameters; the PC lays it out and prints (SumatraPDF / Office / LibreOffice).
- **Wi-Fi printers** (no PC): found with mDNS / DNS-SD (`_ipp._tcp`, `WifiPrinters.kt`, ids like `wifi:192.168.1.50:631/ipp/print`), printed over IPP (`Ipp.kt`, `WifiPrint.kt`).
- **This phone** through the Android print system (`PhonePrint.kt`: ONE print job for all selected files, PDFs keep their real page count). `PcPrintService` also makes LANShare appear as a printer ("LANShare - print on PC") in Android's own print dialog so "Print" in any app can pick it; the finished PDF is copied to `<storage>/LANShare Shared/`.

### 9.2 `pcprint.py` version history (PC side - run the newest file once, it restarts the old service by itself)
| Version | What |
|---|---|
| v9 | fitting options: `fit=fill`, `margin`, `scale`, `align`, `autorot`; `needs_fitting(o)`, `fit_cell()`, `neutral_fit()`; paper size from `paper` |
| v10 | office files (doc docx rtf odt xls xlsx csv ods ppt pptx odp) converted to PDF on the PC (Microsoft Office via COM, else LibreOffice headless) when layout / fitting options are asked for; `/ping` reports `office` |
| v11 | `POST /convert?name=` (office in, PDF out, nothing printed, one at a time; `/ping` `convert: true`) for real office previews |
| v12 | stability: print worker survives any error, `H.timeout = 60`, queue cap 50 (503), free-disk guard (507), unique upload names, picture batches under a lock (last index waits up to 6 s), hourly `cleanup_old` (3 days + 1 GB), one retry on a failing print command (never on timeout), atomic helper `.ps1`, `printer_info(max_age=6)` cache, `GET /jobs`, `/ping` `busy`, `POST /clear` (localhost); window: Test page, Clear queue, Queue folder, Open log |
| v13 | ignores a repeated `key` (no double prints) |
| v15 | `join=1` + batch fields join several files into ONE job; `skip=1` slot |
| v22 | queue screen: every job has id + name; `GET /jobs` adds `waiting:[{id,name,t,printer,copies}]`, `current_id`, `version`; `POST /cancel?id=N` (any LAN phone) cancels a WAITING job (the printing one answers `result:"printing"`) |
| v26 | text inbox (`/p/clip`, `/clip`, `--text` window), UDP beacon listener 48555 (see 10.1) |
| v28 | `GET /screen?pos=N[&lines=K]` for the PC-screen mirror (9.6); `/ping` `screen: true` |
| v32 | print queue restored (v22 had been lost in v28-v31): each queued job gets `qid` + `qt`; `GET /jobs` has `waiting:[{id,name,t,printer,copies}]`, `current_id`, `version`; `POST /cancel?id=N` -> `{ok, result: ok / printing / gone}` (`cancel_waiting()` removes the item from `PRINTQ.queue` under its mutex and answers the phone's request with an error); `/ping` `cancel: true` |

`/ping` reports capability flags (`office`, `convert`, `clip`, `screen`, `busy`); an older file answers 404 on a newer route and the app shows "Update pcprint.py". The note of the screen-mirror session says the v28 file in that zip had **no `waiting` list / `POST /cancel`** (v22 behaviour) although the app's queue screen expects it - verified and restored in v32 (2026-10-28).

### 9.3 What can be printed, and the layout options
- **Conversion on the phone** (`core/PrintPrep.kt`, called from `Jobs.printWork`): webp / heic / heif / avif / ico / wbmp / dng / jfif -> JPEG (`Thumbs.forPrint`); html / htm / xhtml / svg -> PDF (`android/print/WebPdf.kt`: WebView print adapter, no JS, no network; deliberately in package `android.print` because the callback constructors are package-private); ~100 code / config / text extensions -> `.txt`; **unknown / missing extensions are sniffed** (PDF, RTF, OOXML / OpenDocument zip entries, legacy OLE by stream name, decodable picture, HTML / SVG, UTF-16 / UTF-8 text). Video / audio / archives / binaries (`NO` set) and unknown files > 120 MB are refused up front. Office files need Word / Excel / PowerPoint or LibreOffice on the PC for layout options (no native docx renderer on the phone); without a converter the file is printed by its own app (printer + copies only) plus a note.
- **Options** (dialog `PRN_DEF` -> `Jobs.PRINT_KEYS` -> query string -> `pcprint.py parse_opts`): `fit` (shrink / fit / noscale / `fill` = cover and crop), `margin` (0-72 pt, `''` = old default per kind; sent as strings so `'0'` = "None" survives the drop-empty filter), `scale` (10-500 %, overrides `fit`), `align` (`''` centre / `top` / `topleft`), `autorot`, `paper`, duplex / colour (SumatraPDF). With none of the new options set everything behaves as before (old `fit` goes straight to SumatraPDF). Pictures: Scaling = Fit / Fill + Margins (custom scale, position, turn-pages are document-only). Text files: margin = page margin, custom scale = font size. When a paper size is chosen the finished PDF goes to Sumatra as `noscale`. Printers cannot print the last ~3-5 mm, so margin "None" is clipped by hardware. Not tested on a real printer / SumatraPDF (rendered with pdftoppm only).
- **Trim blank edges** (`fit=trim`, pictures only, 2026-10-10): the preview measures each picture's content box (`pvBBox`: outer lines of one flat colour equal to that edge are dropped, 1.5 % safety margin) and sends `crops` (fractions per file); the phone crops the real file before it goes out (`PrintPrep.cropBox` / `trim`, used in `Jobs` PC printing and `PdfPrint.load`; `PhonePrint.prepare`; `WifiPrint.work` / `mergeImages`), then the normal fit logic applies; `pcprint.py` needs no change (unknown `fit` = shrink).
- **One job, no doubles**: `Jobs.printWork` sends `join=1` + `batch`, `idx`, `n` when *Print all files as one job* is on (mixed files, not picture-sheet mode); a file that cannot be sent gets `skip=1` (`skipBatchSlot`); every file carries `key=k<batch>-<index>`. Tested against `pcprint.py` on Linux with a stub.

### 9.4 Previews (`core/PrintPreview.kt`, 2026-10-04)
The dialog's layout preview shows the real pages. Routes `GET /api/pvinfo?dev&path&to`, `/api/pvpage?id&n&w` (JPEG of one page, `PdfRenderer`), `/api/pvfile?id` (converted picture / text). PDF rendered natively; html / svg / unknown / webp-heic-avif converted by `PrintPrep` first; **office files converted by the PC** (`POST /convert`); text / code files drawn in the UI with the same wrapping as `txt_to_pdf` (page / line counts checked identical). Cache `cache/pv/<id>/` (6 documents, wiped at start). **Preview must stay identical to the PC side**: `pvFitK()` <-> `fit_cell()`, `pvSheets()` <-> `images_to_sheets()` (equal on 36 scale cases and 5 picture layouts). The rotate button right of the preview caption (`pvRotBtn`, `pvTurn`) turns the preview 90 deg clockwise - view only, not saved.
First check after a build: `WebPdf.kt` compiles and an `.html` and an `.svg` print.

### 9.5 Wi-Fi printers (IPP)
`Ipp.printerAttrs` asks for `pwg-raster-document-resolution-supported` / `-sheet-back` (`WifiPrinters.P.rasterDpi`, `.sheetBack`). Printers without PDF but with `image/pwg-raster` (e.g. Brother DCP-T830DW) get ALL pages as ONE raster job (`WifiPrint.pwgRaster` / `writeRasterPixels`) so two-sided printing works (before, the per-page JPEG fallback made every page its own sheet). Landscape pages (2-up) are turned 90 deg clockwise; back sides use the printer's sheet-back transform. **Not tested on the printer**: check with 4 numbered pages; if backs come out upside down flip the sheet-back mapping in `pwgRaster`. Printer list is `GET /api/wifiprinters` (50-300 ms scan; `WifiPrinters.start` runs at startup, 13).

### 9.6 Print queue screen and PC screen mirror
- **Print queue** (`pcprint.py` 22+): app `Jobs.pcQueue(dev)` / `pcCancel(dev, id)`, routes `GET /api/pcqueue?dev=` and `POST /api/pccancel?dev=&id=`; `openPrintQueue(pc)` (own `#pq` overlay): Printing now / Waiting (Cancel, second tap within 3 s confirms) / Recent (last 15, tick or cross + reason), refresh every 2 s; entry points: **Print queue ›** link in the PC print dialog and Settings > **Print queue** (last PC used). Queue logic tested on Linux with a stubbed print; Kotlin / Windows not run.
- **PC screen** (button next to Print queue, `pcprint.py` v28; app side **written, not compiled**): `GET /screen?pos=N[&lines=K]` -> `{ok, version, host, pos, reset, lines:[{t,k}], status:{dot green|amber, text, printer, problem, busy, queued}}`; `k` = `ok` (green "printed") / `fail` (red: FAILED / WARNING / CRASH / ERROR / cannot listen) / `dim` (grey: ready / stopped) / `""`. First call (`pos` missing / -1): last 150 lines + `reset:true`; later calls: only new complete lines; log rotation (500 KB) or a viewer > 256 KB behind = `reset:true` + tail. Stopped service = nothing answers ("PC not reachable"); old file = 404 ("update pcprint.py (needs version 28 or newer)"). Window and endpoint share `log_tag()` / `status_parts()` / `ips_label()`. Privacy: the log has file names and IPs - visible to anything that can reach port 8799 (as `/jobs`); read-only. App: `Jobs.pcScreen(dev, pos)` (404 -> `{ok:true, old:true}`), route `"pcscreen"` in `Routes.kt` (not in `InProc.ROUTES`), `openPcScreen(pc)` in ui.html (own `#pcs` overlay reusing the `#pq` look, polls 1 s, follows the newest line only while scrolled to the bottom, keeps 1000 lines; CSS `.pcb .pcst .pcd .pcl .pcln`). Not run: the Tk window after the refactor (`py_compile` only).
- Checklist: copy `pcprint.py` v28, run it; app -> print dialog -> **PC screen ›**: last ~150 lines at once, status line matches the PC window; print something -> new lines within ~1 s ("printed" green, failures red; scroll up = view stops jumping); stop the service -> "PC not reachable" after ~2 s, start again -> recovers; old pcprint -> "Update pcprint.py"; close the sheet -> polling stops.
- Ideas: SSE / long poll instead of 1 s polling; "Copy log"; "Clear queue" in the app (`POST /clear` is localhost-only).

### 9.7 Print space (drawer > Print space)

**Status (2026-10-23).** All parts written. Kotlin of Print space was never confirmed on a device in these notes (CI builds the app; check the Status board). JVM / node / Chromium tests exist for the maths and UI helpers (list at the end). Detailed per-part history (risk spots, per-part test lists, old plans) was removed from this file on 2026-10-23 - it is in the zips delivered before `pcshare-handover-part1.zip` (and in git).

**Goal.** Send pictures (and PDF pages) from the gallery / file browser / viewer / share sheet into a holding area, lay them out on one or both sides of sheets (grid or free placement), preview with the real renderer, print to PC, Wi-Fi IPP or this phone.

**Design decisions (still valid)**
- **Copies, not links.** Pictures are copied to `<root>/.LANShare Print/<id>/<name>` (`core/PrintSpace.kt`, manifest `items.json`, `.nomedia`). Cap 500 pictures, 99 copies each. Originals can change or vanish without affecting a pending print.
- **Reuse the print pipeline.** Stored files are normal local virtual paths; `doPrint(preset, cx)` and `printOptions` work on them. The layout editor renders ONE PDF and hands it to `doPrint`.
- **Screen = `#gal` overlay** (like image / video viewers), so it must stay in the `nlLay` holes and the lists that hide native list / chrome / search / swipe (6.1). One drawer `dItem` above *Recycle bin* opens it (`openPrintSpace()`); kill switch `localStorage ls_ps='0'` stops every way in (drawer, `psOK()`, `openPrintSpace()`, `psAdd()`); the native viewer / share entries have no switch.
- **Server is the source of truth for validity**, the page for geometry during a gesture (local copy, redraw of the touched element, ONE request when the finger lifts).
- **Both sides.** Each sheet has front and back lists; back slots are stored as READ; `backSlot` / `behind[]` gives the slot physically behind a front slot (flip long / short). Printers without duplex: print fronts, prompt, print backs.

**Source map**

| Area | Files |
|---|---|
| Store, undo, settings, PDF intake | `core/PrintSpace.kt` (`add`, `addStream`, `addPdf`, `adopt`, `park` / undo, `cfg`), `GET/POST /api/ps` |
| Layout model (pure Kotlin, no `android.*`) | `core/SheetLayout.kt` |
| Wire format | `core/PsLayoutJson.kt` (org.json only), `core/PsLayoutApi.kt` (android.*, `POST /api/pslayout`) |
| Renderer | `core/PsRenderGeom.kt` (pure), `core/PrintSpaceRender.kt` (`POST /api/psrender`, `GET /api/pspage`) |
| Recent prints (sessions) | `core/PrintSessions.kt`, `GET/POST /api/pssess`, `GET /api/pssprev` |
| Printing paths | `ui.html` `doPrint` / `printOptions` / `wifiOptions`, `PhonePrint.kt`, `pcprint.py` |
| UI | `ui.html`: `openPrintSpace()`, `psLayout()` (editor), `dFree()` (free stage), `psSettings()`, `printRecentPs()` |

#### Store and API (`/api/ps`)
- `GET` -> `{items:[{id,name,size,from,at,copies,rot,path}], count, bytes, max, undo?}`. `POST` ops: `add {dev,paths}` (-> `{added, skipped:[{name,why}]}`), `rm {ids}`, `clear`, `move {id,to}`, `set {id | ids, copies?, rot?, rotBy?}`, `undo`, `cfg`. **Route rule:** a route name must branch on `ex.method` (a missing guard once made every POST just return the list).
- **Intake**: gallery hold bar and file-browser hold menu *To Print space* (`psPic` / `psOK` / `psAdd`), image viewer *More > To Print space*, share-sheet alias `.PrintSpaceShareAlias` ("LANShare Print space", `image/*` and PDF, `PrintSpace.addStream`), pictures from other devices (`dev != local`: streamed from `Jobs.ep(dev)`, max 60 MB each, `from = "<dev>:<path>"`). Press and hold starts selecting, a tap opens the picture (tap ticks once a selection exists). Selection order = TAP order (`gi()` / `selItems()`), cells show the tap number (`gNum`).
- **PDF pages**: each page becomes an ordinary JPEG item `"<name> p<N>.jpg"` (`PdfRenderer`, 200 dpi, long side <= 3000 px, `MAX_PDF_PAGES` 100). One page = one picture, so copies / turn / thumbnails / undo / layout all work unchanged.
- **Undo for Remove / Remove all**: folders are MOVED to `.undo/<id>/` (`park()`), `undoList` is memory only, one step; the screen shows *N pictures removed* + Undo for 10 s.
- **Multi-select bar**: Print / Copies (1-99) / Rotate (90 from each picture's own rotation) / Remove. Tiles show the rotation by CSS.
- **Settings card** (`psSettings()`): *Remove pictures after* Never / 7 / 30 / 90 days, *Storage limit* none / 200 MB / 1 GB / 5 GB (`settings.json`, `sweep()` from `load()` / `json()`), *Recent prints kept* 5 / 12 / 25 / 50 and *size* 200 MB / 500 MB / 2 GB / none. The sweep can remove pictures a saved layout still names: the editor then shows empty slots.

#### Layout model (`SheetLayout.kt`, units = PDF points, origin top-left)
- **Spec**: `paper` (A3 / A4 / A5 / Letter / Legal, unknown = A4), `orient` AUTO / PORTRAIT / LANDSCAPE, `cols x rows` (presets 1x1, 1x2, 2x2, 2x3, 3x3, custom up to 8 per axis), `margin` (14 pt, 0-72), `gap` (8, 0-40), `fit` FIT / FILL, `flip` long / short, `autoRot`, `reverse`, plus `free`, `safe` (14, 0-72), `keep` (true), `keepGap` (6, 0-40) for free placement. Every field has a default and `clean()` clamps, so named-argument `Spec(...)` calls keep compiling.
- **Pic** = `{w, h}` as the picture LOOKS (EXIF applied) + `rot` + `copies`. **Plan** = `{portrait, sheets}`, `Sheet` = `front` / `back` slot lists (picture index or null) + `ff` / `fb` free lists.
- `autoFill(spec, pics, mode)`: FRONT_ONLY / BOTH_SIDES / PAIRS (0+1, 2+3 ...; copies of the front repeat the pair). AUTO orientation uses the same score rule as `pvSheets`. Editing ops are immutable: `assign`, `swap`, `placeBehind`, `addSheet`, `trim`, `reflow`, `removeSheet`, `slotBoxes`.
- `compose(spec, pics, plan)` -> `Layout{portrait, sw, sh, grid, pages, score}`; pages in PRINT order (sheet 0 front, sheet 0 back ...); back pages exist for EVERY sheet as soon as one sheet has a back picture.
- **Parity**: single-sided FIT / FILL equals `pvSheets()` (ui.html) and `images_to_sheets()` (`pcprint.py`) - `tools/sheet_layout_mirror_test.py`.

#### Wire format (`PsLayoutJson`, `POST /api/pslayout`)
- Request `{op, spec{...}, plan{portrait, sheets:[{f:[id|null], b:[id|null], ff?, fb?}]}, ...}`; the UI keeps spec + plan and sends both with every call. Answer `{ok, spec(cleaned), plan, layout{portrait, sw, sh, cols, rows, slots[{x,y,w,h}], behind[], pages[{sheet, side, cells[{slot,id,rot,dw,dh}], free[{id,cx,cy,dw,dh,rot}]}]}, placed{id:n}, sheets}`. A cell = picture drawn `dw x dh` pt UNTURNED, turned by `rot` about the slot centre, clipped to the slot box. Free cells: same but centred on `cx, cy`, no clip.
- Ops: `view`, `fill {mode}`, `assign`, `swap`, `behind`, `addsheet`, `rmsheet`, `trim`, `tofree`, `togrid`, `fset {sheet, side, items:[{id,cx,cy,w,rot,st?}]}`, `fadd {sheet, side, id, cx?, cy?}`. Request preamble (clean spec, picture list, plan or front fill) lives in `base()`; `resolve()` = same without the op (used by the renderer).
- `PsLayoutApi` reads sizes with `BitmapFactory` bounds + `ExifInterface` (cached per id; unreadable header = 4000 x 3000).

#### Layout editor (`psLayout()` in ui.html)
- **Entry**: *Layout* button in the Print space header (hidden while empty). State inside `psLayout`: `SP` spec, `PL` plan, `LY` layout, `PICS`, `PLACED`, `MODE`, `sheet`, `side`, `selPic`, `selSlot`; saved as `ls_ps_lay = {spec, plan, mode}` after every answer (a layout the server refuses is dropped).
- **Calls** are serialized (`call()` chain); the cleaned spec is adopted only when no newer call waits (quick +/- taps add up); a refused setting toasts and keeps the old one.
- **Settings card** (tune button): paper, per sheet 1 / 2 / 4 / 6 / 9 / Custom, orientation, fit / fill, flip, margin, gap, turn to fit, order, Auto-fill (front only / both sides / pairs), Remove empty sheets, Placement Grid | Free, saved layouts. Sheet strip with +/delete, Front / Back tabs, tray of pictures.
- **Grid gestures**: tap picture then slot = assign; tap filled slot then another = swap; hold 380 ms and drag tray -> slot (assign), slot -> slot (swap), slot -> tray (clear). Selection bar: copies +/-, Turn, Clear, *Back side*.
- **Preview / Print**: header buttons. `prep()` runs after every layout answer (350 ms debounce) and calls `POST /api/psrender {op:"prepare"}`; Preview shows `/api/pspage` JPEGs with a page strip; Print (`printNow()`) waits for queued calls, warns about pictures not on a sheet, then renders the PDF (`op:"pdf"` -> job -> normal progress bubble, Cancel works).
- **Rotate view** (`vr`, `vrBtn`, `vrWrap`): view-only turn of the big page.
- **Saved layouts**: *+ Save this layout* (name <= 24 chars, max 20, `localStorage` `ls_ps_presets`) keeps only `PSP_KEYS` (spec fields) + auto-fill mode; older presets without a new key keep the current value. Free placement is kept in presets too (item templates `tpl`, 12a).

#### Renderer (`PrintSpaceRender`, `PsRenderGeom`)
- Draws a layout exactly as the editor shows it: slot box clipped, turned about its centre, unturned picture `dw x dh`; free items after the cells, no slot clip. One picture decoded at a time (`needPx` / `sample`, power-of-two `inSampleSize`, <= 3000 px); default 200 dpi (96-300), `autoDpi` lowers it when the raw size estimate passes 400 MB.
- `prepare` -> `{ok, id, pages, sheets, sw, sh, duplex, list:[{sheet,side}]}` (id = hash of spec + composed layout; 12 prepared layouts in memory, LRU). `pspage?id&n&w` -> JPEG q85, `n` in print order, `w` 120-1100, two at a time. `pdf` -> `{job}`; one PDF, one page per layout page; one render at a time; request fields `only: front|back` and `reverse: true` for manual duplex; the renderer keeps only the newest 4 PDFs.
- Free items are clipped to the safe rectangle as a last resort (plans that never went through `setFree`).

#### Printing paths
- **Sides preset**: `doPrint(preset, cx)` reads `cx.opts` and returns the printer used; `printOptions` / `wifiOptions` start from `pre` (duplex = flip edge or `off`). PC: `pcprint.py` v28 (`duplex` off / long / short, `duplex_swap.txt`). Wi-Fi IPP: `sides=two-sided-long-edge` / `-short-edge`, raster `sheet-back` flips. This phone: `printHereMany` gets `duplex` -> `PrintAttributes.Builder.setDuplexMode`.
- **Manual two-sided** (printer without duplex): Print asks *Does the printer print on both sides by itself?*; "No" renders `front` then `back` (dialog in between, *Last sheet first* = `reverse`).
- **Printer margins**: Wi-Fi IPP asks `media-*-margin-supported` (`Ipp.printerAttrs`, `WifiPrinters.marginsOf` = per edge the smallest NON-ZERO value); chip *From printer...* in the safe-margin chips uses `/api/printer`; PC side already reported `hard`.

#### Free placement (spec.free)
- **Model**: `Free(pic, cx, cy, w, rot, st)` = fractions of the sheet (centre, width of the UNTURNED picture) + clockwise degrees + stretch `st` (see below); list order = stacking order; `Sheet.ff` / `fb`; the picture's own turn adds to the item's turn. `fset` replaces one side; `fadd` places a new item (server picks size / spot); `tofree` / `togrid` switch mode (*Auto-arrange* = fill then `tofree`).
- **Stage** (`dFree()`, `.lyfp`, items `.lyf`): thumbnails with CSS transforms (percent sizes: `w`, `w*a*sw/sh`), local copy `FRL` / `FEL`, one `fset` on pointer up (`fCommit`). Gesture modes: `m` move, `c` corner dot, `s` side dot, `r` rotate knob, `p` pinch, `bg`, `x` (ignore until all fingers up). Snapping: centre lines 1.2 %, angle 15 degrees within 3.
- **Handles on the selected picture**: four corner dots (resize with the OPPOSITE corner fixed, shape kept, grab offset kept), two **side dots** (mid left / right, pill shaped), and a round rotate knob (top, else bottom / left / right whichever stays on the page; left / right sit 46 px out so they clear the side dots).
- **Side dots (2026-10-23)**: `fSide` keeps the opposite side and the height, changes only the width. Stored as `st` = horizontal stretch (1 = own proportions, 0.2-5); `a` (height / width) already includes it (the server `dh / dw` carries it), so every helper that reads `f.a` keeps working. After a side drag `st = st0 * a0 / a`. Corner drag, pinch and `fClamp` shrink scale uniformly (stretch kept). `st` is sent in every `fset` / snapshot / template and read by `PsLayoutJson` (written only when != 1); Kotlin uses `stOf(f)` in `freeCell`, `freeBox`, `freePoly`, `freeHit`, `clampFree`. Renderers draw `dw x dh` separately, so no renderer change.
- **Control bar** (single picture; the owner removed several buttons on 2026-10-23 - see `dCtl` for the current set): Undo / Redo (stacks `FU` / `FR`, 30 deep, `fsnap`, `fpush`, `fundo`), Multi, Fit page, Fit width, Delete; helpers still in code: Align, Spread, numeric *Rot / size...* (`15, 60`), Center, To front, Copy. Front **ghost** on the Back tab (22 %, dashed, mirrored about the flip edge, not touchable).
- **Printer safe margin** (`spec.safe`): the TURNED box of every picture stays inside paper minus `safe` on all four sides (`fSafeR`, `fClamp`: shrink first with shape kept, then move; idempotent). Chips Off / 3 / 5 / 6 / 10 mm (0 / 9 / 14 / 18 / 28 pt) + current + *From printer...*. Red dashed frame + hatched strip (`fFrame`). Loaded items are pulled in with ONE quiet `fset` (not an undo step, max 2 in a row, `fixN`). Server enforces the same maths (`SheetLayout.clampFree` in `setFree` / `addFree` / `gridToFree`).
- **Keep apart** (`spec.keep`, default ON, `keepGap` chips 0 / 2 / 4 / 8 mm): exact oriented-box overlap test (`fHit`, separating axis, gap grown); move / corner / side / rotate / pinch stop at the neighbour (path sampled every ~2 px, `fStop` / `fMoveTo` slide along); neighbours that ALREADY overlapped never block (`kI`); buttons refuse with the toast "Pictures cannot overlap (Keep apart is on)" (`fkRevert`); tray add finds a free spot (`fSpot`, server `freeSpot`; none = "No room on this side for another picture"); turning the setting on offers *Spread them* (`fPack`, shelf packing, one common shrink factor); overlapping pictures get a red outline while the setting is on. Server: `setFree` refuses a NEW overlap.
- **Behaviour to know**: pictures cannot leave the paper in free mode even with margin *Off*; turning is only by the knob (the old corner dot did resize + turn).

#### Recent prints keep everything (sessions)
- A *session* = snapshot folder `<root>/.LANShare Print/.sessions/<sid>/` with `session.json` (spec, plan, mode, only, rev, printer `to` / `toName` / `opts`, names, pictures, pages, `hasPdf`, `bytes`, `ok`), `pics/<picid>/<name>` and the rendered PDF + page previews. Saved by `phAdd` -> `phSave` right after the render (fire and forget; `{sid, pending}` answer, built in a daemon thread in `<sid>.tmp`); `phMark` updates `ok`. `sid` validated `[0-9a-z]{6,24}`; a plan without pictures is refused.
- `GET /api/pssess` (list), `GET /api/pssprev?sid&n`, `POST /api/pssess {op: save | get | restore | pdf | mark | rm | clear | cfg}`. **Restore** puts pictures back under their OLD ids (`PrintSpace.adopt`) so the stored plan stays valid.
- UI `printRecentPs()`: rows with up to 3 lazy previews, title, printer / date / pictures / pages / size / two-sided, **Edit** (restore -> `ls_ps_lay`, printer + options restored, `openPrintSpace(); psLayout()`), **Print again** (only with `hasPdf`; missing PDF falls back to Edit), cross (second tap within 3 s). Old builds / errors fall back to `printRecent(true, true)` (history entries with `h.ps`).
- Not done: sessions for ordinary prints, de-duplication of copies between sessions (cap bounds the space), Chromium test of the list DOM.

#### Not done on purpose
Crop, per-slot rotation, snapping to neighbours, black-and-white / crop marks / headers, vector PDF pages (raster only), built-in layouts, history for *This phone*, a printer "test sheet" for duplex stacking, zoom / pan in Preview, JPEG pass-through in the PDF.

#### Device checklist (Print space, all open; stop at the first failure)
1. Drawer > Print space; add from gallery, file browser, viewer, share sheet (image and PDF); tap opens the picture, press and hold selects; remove + Undo; Copies / Rotate on a selection.
2. Layout 2 x 2 A4: auto-fill (front / both sides / pairs), drag / swap, Preview pages match the editor, Print to PC, Wi-Fi printer and this phone; two-sided preset and manual duplex (front, dialog, back, *Last sheet first*).
3. Free placement: add by tap and drag from the tray; move, pinch, four corner dots (opposite corner stays), two side dots (height unchanged, stops at frame / neighbours), rotate knob (snaps 15 degrees), Undo / Redo, Fit page / Fit width, Back tab ghost; margin chip 10 mm / Off; Keep apart stops at neighbours, *Spread them* offers on overlap; Preview and Print show stretched pictures.
4. Save layout and use it; Recent prints > Edit restores pictures, spec and printer; Print again; Select several prints and Remove; settings card limits (remove after N days, storage limit).
5. First compile / device run of everything marked "not compiled" in section 2: paste the first compiler error.

#### Tests
`SheetLayout.kt` + `tools/sheetlayout_selftest/Main.kt` and `tools/psrender_geom_selftest/Main.kt` (kotlinc, JVM, ALL OK); `tools/sheet_layout_mirror_test.py`, `tools/psclamp_14c_mirror_test.py` (Kotlin maths vs the real ui.html helpers, 19,968 checks); `node tools/psfree_12b_test.js`, `psfree_12c_test.js`, `psfree_14a_test.js` (100,023 checks), `psfree_14b_test.js` (4,356 checks), `psess_13c_test.js`; `tools/pslayout_ui_smoke_test.js` (79 checks, jsdom / Chromium), `tools/psscreen_ui_smoke_chromium.py` (14 checks). No automated test yet for the free-stage gestures (incl. side dots) or the Recent prints list DOM; `PsLayoutJson` has no own JVM cases.

## 10. Text sharing and Phone Explorer

### 10.1 Text sharing (replace WhatsApp for text / links)
**Status 2026-10-15**: code complete on phone + PC, Kotlin NOT compiled, NOT device-tested; branch `feat/text-inbox` of `topgun1111/pcshare`, nothing pushed at the time of the note. First step if it is not merged: copy the zip contents over the repo (or `git apply text-sharing.patch` - that patch is now **stale**: its content is already in the code and it no longer applies cleanly), push, open the PR, fix compiler errors from the Actions log.

**Already existed (do not rebuild)**: `/p/clip` (receive) + `/api/sendclip` (send) + `RemoteFs.pushClip` + `ClipPush.kt` (on-screen box + heads-up notification, links auto-open) + the share-sheet alias `.SendShareAlias` ("LANShare Send").

| Piece | Files | Behaviour |
|---|---|---|
| Inbox store | `core/Notes.kt` | `<filesDir>/inbox.json`, max 300 items / 2 MB, `add / all / remove / clear`, `listeners`, `visible`; fed by `ClipPush.received` (in) and `Routes sendclip` + `TextSend.send` (out) |
| Inbox screen | `InboxActivity.kt`, `Bridge.openInbox()`, drawer item | native chat-like list; tap = copy, long-press = copy / open link / share / delete; type or paste + Send; "To:" line picks the DEFAULT device; ⋮ menu: Auto-copy, Clipboard sync, Clear |
| Default target | `Cfg.defTarget()` (`def_target`), `TextSend.target()` | default if online; else (no default) the only online device; else null -> picker |
| Share-sheet fast path | `ClipSendActivity.kt` (manifest SEND text/plain, label "LANShare Text") | invisible activity, sends shared text to the target at once; picker only if several online and no default |
| Direct Share | `Shortcuts.kt`, `res/xml/shortcuts.xml`, meta-data on MainActivity | one dynamic shortcut per online device (default first), category `com.lanshare.app.category.SEND_TEXT`, share-target class `ClipSendActivity`; re-synced every ~10 s from `LanShareService.noteTick` and on inbox open; also launcher long-press |
| Quick Settings tile | `SendClipTile.kt` | tap -> `ClipSendActivity` (needs window focus to read the clipboard on Android 10+) -> default device; subtitle = default device (API 29+); `startActivityAndCollapse(PendingIntent)` on API 34+ |
| Notification quick-reply | `QuickSendReceiver.kt`, `LanShareService.buildNote` | foreground notification: inline "Send text" (RemoteInput) -> default device, "Clipboard", "Stop"; `LanShareService.refresh()` re-posts to clear the input |
| Auto-copy (receive) | `Cfg.autoCopy()`, `ClipPush.received` | incoming text written to the clipboard in the background |
| Clipboard sync (send) | `ClipSync.kt`, `App.kt` (Application class, manifest `android:name=".App"`) | opt-in; while >= 1 LANShare screen is visible, copied text is pushed silently (`quiet:true`) to the default device; skips IS_SENSITIVE clips, blank / > 100 KB, echoes (`mark`); **foreground only** (Android 10+ rule); the receiver honours `quiet` only if ITS Auto-copy is on (prevents silent clipboard hijack by any LAN device) |

Protocol change: `/p/clip` body gains optional `"quiet": true` (old receivers ignore it), and `id` + `port` of the sender.

**PC side (`pcprint.py` v26 + `core/PcText.kt`)**: `POST /p/clip` (also `/clip`) stores texts in `%APPDATA%\LANSharePrint\inbox.json` (max 300 / 2 MB), optional copy to the clipboard (PowerShell `Set-Clipboard`; setting "Copy received text automatically", default off, also the opt-in for quiet pushes) and opens the **LANShare Text** window (setting "Open this window on new text", default on, at most every 5 s). Localhost only (the window): `GET /clip?since=N`, `POST /clip/send {to,text}`, `/clip/delete {id}`, `/clip/settings {autocopy,popup}`; other LAN devices cannot read the inbox. Devices to send to: the service listens for the phones' UDP beacons (port 48555, `SO_REUSEADDR`) and keeps those heard in the last 90 s; a phone that sends text here registers itself; `/clip/send` only talks to devices in that list. Window: `pcprint.py --text` or the **Text** button (single instance, named mutex): list (double-click = copy), Copy / Open link / Delete / Clear all, device drop-down + box + Send (Ctrl+Enter), the two switches. `/ping` has `"clip": true`. Setup adds a UDP firewall rule `LANSharePrint-Beacons` (48555, LocalSubnet + tailnet) - run the new file once so Windows asks. Tested on Linux with a stub phone; the Tk window only against mocked tkinter; Windows clipboard / popup / firewall not run.
**Phone side (`core/PcText.kt`, `TextSend`)**: a PC is not a LANShare peer, so every ~30 s (on demand from `TextSend.online()`) the known addresses (devices, saved SMB hosts, pins, ARP neighbours) get `GET :8799/ping`; a reply with `clip:true` makes that host a target "<PC> (PC)", id `pc:<ip>`, port 8799, shown wherever phones are (inbox, picker, default device, Direct Share, tile, share sheet) because `TextSend.online()` / `find()` merge both lists; `RemoteFs.pushClip` posts to its `/p/clip` unchanged. Old pcprint (404) -> "pcprint.py ... is older than v26". The phone must be on the same subnet as the PC (or both on the tailnet).

**Files** - new: `core/PcText.kt`, `App.kt`, `ClipSendActivity.kt`, `ClipSync.kt`, `InboxActivity.kt`, `QuickSendReceiver.kt`, `SendClipTile.kt`, `Shortcuts.kt`, `TextSend.kt`, `core/Notes.kt`, `res/xml/shortcuts.xml`; modified: `AndroidManifest.xml`, `ui.html`, `ClipPush.kt`, `LanShareService.kt`, `MainActivity.kt`, `core/Cfg.kt`, `core/RemoteFs.kt`, `core/Routes.kt`, `res/values/themes.xml`, `pcprint.py`.
**Compile / device suspects**: `LanShareService` companion access to private members, `Intent.EXTRA_SHORTCUT_ID` (API 29) with minSdk 24, `ShortcutInfoCompat` / `IconCompat`, `Tile.subtitle` (API 29 guard); `ClipSendActivity` is `noHistory` + translucent `Theme.LANShare.Clear` - verify window focus from tile, notification and share sheet; Direct Share category must match `shortcuts.xml`.
**Device checklist**: inbox send / receive both ways; default device persists; tile; notification inline reply; share text from Chrome -> "LANShare Text" and -> a device name (Direct Share); Auto-copy with the app closed; Clipboard sync on (app open) copies on the other phone only when its Auto-copy is on; password-manager copy NOT synced; inbox survives restart. PC: phone -> PC (window opens, text listed, Copy works, link opens), PC -> phone (device appears within ~2 s of opening LANShare on the phone; text lands in the inbox), auto-copy on / off, Windows asks for the UDP rule once.
**Not done / ideas**: long-press a device chip to set the default; unread badge; file attachments in the inbox; Settings row in ui.html for the switches; consider removing the "LANShare Send" alias (three LANShare text entries in the share sheet: "LANShare" print `*/*`, "LANShare Send" alias, "LANShare Text").

### 10.2 Phone Explorer (phone storage in Windows Explorer, one tap)
**Status 2026-10-18**: coded, NOT compiled, NOT run on a device or on Windows. Drawer row **Phone Explorer** = one tap: `peOpenOnPc()` (ui.html) starts the phone's FTP share if needed, broadcasts a search for PCs (`GET /api/pcfind` -> `PePc.find`, UDP 41235, answered by `phone_explorer.py` on the PC), picks the PC (last used by name; one PC = at once, several = one-time picker) and calls `POST /api/pcphone?ip&hport&port&user&pin&name` -> `PePc.open` -> `POST http://PC:8798/open` -> the PC tool runs `explorer.exe ftp://user:PIN@<phone ip>:2121/`. Nothing to type; `pcprint.py` is NOT involved. The gear on the row opens the settings sheet `openPhoneExplorer()`: Start / Stop, address, PIN, Copy link, New PIN, QR, Read-only, Start automatically, shared folders, port, **Open on PC**.
- **Files**: `pe/FtpServer.kt` (pure JDK FTP server, port 2121, user `phone`, 6-digit PIN, LAN clients only), `pe/PeDiscovery.kt` (UDP 41234, answers the old PC tool's search), `pe/PePrefs.kt`, `pe/PeService.kt` (foreground service, notification 21, locks, `PeBoot` receiver), `pe/PePc.kt` (find / open PC), `pe/PeTls.kt` (FTPS option: one self-signed RSA-2048 certificate, 10 years, made on first use and kept in `filesDir` as `pe_tls_key.pk8` + `pe_tls_cert.der`, so an accepted fingerprint stays valid; **no handover note beyond the file header**). `MainActivity.Bridge`: `peState / peStart / peStop / peSet / peNewPin / peQr / peGrant`. `Routes.kt`: `pcfind`, `pcphone`. Manifest: `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_SPECIAL_USE`, `RECEIVE_BOOT_COMPLETED`, service `.pe.PeService`, receiver `.pe.PeBoot`. Gradle: `com.google.zxing:core:3.5.3`.
- **PC**: `tools/phone_explorer.py` (Tkinter + tray; UDP responder 41235, HTTP `/open` on 8798, LAN clients only, user / PIN validated, saves the phone in its list, opens Explorer; keep it running, allow it once in the Windows firewall); the .exe is built by `.github/workflows/build-phone-explorer-exe.yml`. Smoke tests: `tools/ftp_smoke_test.py`, `tools/ftps_smoke_test.py`.
- **Limits**: needs All-files access (the first tap opens that setting); Android 11+ keeps `Android/data` and `Android/obb` closed; router "client isolation" blocks everything; the share is independent of the LANShare service but the LANShare notification Stop still kills the whole app; a port change needs Stop + Start (read-only and the folder list apply at once).

### 10.3 Remote command (admin, off by default, password set on the PC) - owner's own devices only
**Update 2026-10-30 (owner request): all PIN authentication removed.** `/exec` on the PC has no PIN; the only gate is the LAN-only check + the ON/OFF button ("Remote commands: ON/OFF") in the `phone_explorer.py` window (config `exec_enabled`, off by default). `PePc.exec(ip,hport,cmd)`, `Routes` `pcexec` and `ui.html openPcExec()` no longer take / ask for a PIN. While ON, any device on the LAN can run commands as the PC program (admin if elevated). Not compiled / not device-tested yet; the PIN lines below are historical.
**Status 2026-10-30**: PC side (`tools/phone_explorer.py`) finished and `py_compile`-checked this session. Phone side (`pe/PePc.kt` `exec()`, `Routes.kt` `pcexec`, `ui.html` `openPcExec()`) is coded but **NOT compiled, NOT device-tested** - next session: build the APK, fix compile errors, then the checklist below.
- **What it does**: "Remote command" button inside the Phone Explorer settings sheet -> picks a PC (reuses `/api/pcfind`), enters that PC's own "remote command PIN" (separate from the FTP PIN) + a command line, `POST /api/pcexec?ip&hport&pin&cmd` -> `PePc.exec()` -> `POST http://PC:8798/exec?pin&cmd` -> shows stdout / stderr / exit code (truncated at 64 KB).
- **PC side** (`phone_explorer.py`): new window section "Remote commands" - PIN (auto-generated 6-digit, Copy / New PIN) + "Allow the phone to run commands on this PC" checkbox (**off by default**); `/exec` on the existing port 8798 checks LAN-only + `exec_enabled` + `hmac.compare_digest` PIN match, then `subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, timeout=60)`. **Admin**: on launch the program now tries `relaunch_elevated()` (one UAC prompt via `ShellExecute runas`; skip with `--no-admin`); title bar and a label show whether it is running elevated. `set_startup()` now prefers a Task Scheduler entry (`/RL HIGHEST`, needs the process to already be elevated to create it) so later logons start pre-elevated with no repeated prompt; falls back to the old Run-key (re-prompts every login) when not elevated.
- **Files touched**: `tools/phone_explorer.py` (admin helpers, Task Scheduler autostart, `/exec`, UI section), `app/.../pe/PePc.kt` (`exec()`, `ExecResult`), `app/.../core/Routes.kt` (`"pcexec"` case), `app/src/main/assets/ui.html` (`openPcExec()`, button in `openPhoneExplorer()`). No manifest change needed.
- **Risk, flagged to the owner**: this is a full remote-shell endpoint on the user's own LAN tool; it is PIN-gated and off by default, but anyone who learns the PIN and reaches port 8798 can run anything the PC process can. Keep the PIN private; turn the checkbox off when not using it.
- **Device checklist (next session)**: APK compiles; on PC, UAC prompt appears and title shows "(Administrator)"; toggle on, PIN shown; phone lists the PC, enters PIN, runs e.g. `whoami /all` and sees admin output; wrong PIN -> "wrong PIN"; toggle off -> "Remote commands are turned off on this PC"; "Start with Windows" while elevated creates the scheduled task (`schtasks /Query /TN PhoneExplorer`) and the next logon starts with no UAC prompt.

## 11. Image search and OCR

### 11.1 Image recognition search ("AI search")
**Goal**: the web portal's search on the phone: CLIP ViT-B/32 image vectors (visual search: CLIP text vector, cosine >= 0.20, top 60) and OCR text search ("Metin Arama": word / substring with Turkish folding). Reference files (`arama.php`, `clip_worker.js`) are not in the repo. **State**: code complete; **the first real device test is still open** (checklist below). Settings > Image search; drawer entry "Image search".

**Backend (`core/ImgSearch.kt`)**
- `interface Engine { id; canOcr; embedImage(jpeg); embedText(q); ocr(file); release() }`, `@Volatile var engine`; until set, `start` answers 400 "image search engine is not installed yet" (text search still works on stored text).
- Discovery walks the storage root (jpg / jpeg / png / webp / bmp / heic / heif, skips `Android/` and hidden dirs), virtual paths like `/DCIM/Camera/x.jpg`. Incremental index keyed by path + mtime + size; vanished files dropped; unreadable pictures `failed` (retried on change or `force`); engine id change = index discarded. Stored in `<filesDir>/imgsearch/index.json` + `vec.bin` (float32 LE, 512 per entry; `.tmp` then rename, manifest last); checkpoint every 100 pictures; daemon thread `imgsearch`. CLIP input = `Thumbs.make(file)` (600 px, EXIF-rotated).
- `visual(q)`: dot product >= 0.20, top 60. `text(q, whole)`: lower-case(tr) + `ı -> i`; whole word = `(?<![\p{L}\p{N}])q(?![\p{L}\p{N}])`, else substring.
- Robustness: `Engine.release()` after every scan; `IOException` / `OutOfMemoryError` = bad picture, any other exception (ONNX `OrtException`) is logged and 5 in a row abort the scan ("Remove the model in Settings and download it again"); `uninstall` refused during a scan; model download sends `Accept-Encoding: identity`.
- **Auto-scan**: `startAutoWatch(ctx)` (after the first screen, 13) = `ContentObserver` on `MediaStore.Images` (20 s debounce) + 10 min poll; only after a first scan, engine installed, permission ok, not during a scan; deferred 15 min when battery < 20 % and not charging; OCR only if the index already has text. Settings `img_auto` (default on), `img_auto_chg`; `POST /api/imgs {op:"auto", on, charging}`.
- **Folders**: Settings > Image search folders (`openExcluded()`): **Only these folders** (empty = whole phone) and **Skip these folders** (wins); `lanshare.json` `img_incl` / `img_excl`; `isOut()` drops out-of-scope pictures from index and results. **Album AI search**: `isView(sdir, sname)` sends `dir`; `visual/text(..., dir)` keep pictures in `dir` or below.
- **API**: `GET /api/imgs` -> `{state idle|running|error, engine, ocr, total, done, indexed, withText, failed, count, msg, error, model{installed, installing, msg, error, bytes, totalBytes}, msPerPic, auto, included[], excluded[]}`; `POST /api/imgs {op: start{ocr,force} | cancel | clear | install | uninstall | selftest | visual{q,dir?} | text{q,mode:word|part,dir?} | auto | included | excluded}`. Not in `InProc.ROUTES`.

**Engine (`core/ClipEngine.kt`)**
- Dependencies `onnxruntime-android:1.18.0` + ML Kit text recognition; APK ~110 MB without an ABI filter, `abiFilters += "arm64-v8a"` fixes it. ML Kit Translate was removed 2026-10-10 (APK 25 -> 45 MB).
- `install()` (thread `clip-download`) downloads once into `<filesDir>/models/clip/`: `vision.onnx`, `text.onnx` (quantized), `vocab.json`, `merges.txt` from `huggingface.co/Xenova/clip-vit-base-patch32` (~150 MB, manual redirects, `.part` then rename, no resume; **file names / sizes unverified**). `tryLoad(ctx)` in `Core.start`.
- `Impl` id `clip-vit-b32-q8-v1` (**bump when preprocessing changes**): lazy ORT sessions (2 threads), output `image_embeds` / `text_embeds` else first `[1][512]`, L2-normalised; image preprocessing = shortest side 224, centre crop, mean / std, NCHW. `ClipTokenizer` = CLIP byte-level BPE, `[49406 ... 49407]`, max 77; written from memory, **not run against the real vocab**.
- **OCR for the text index**: `Impl.ocr()` uses `TiledOcr(AutoOcr)` (11.2), 150 s per picture; gallery-only `TiledOcr.skipTilesIfEmpty`. Pictures indexed earlier keep their old text until **Rescan all**.
- Diagnostics: `{op:"selftest"}` -> `catIds` / `catIdsOk` (must equal `[49406,320,1125,539,320,2368,49407]`), `selfDot` (~1.0), `catVsDog`, `catVsKitten`, `textMs`; the scan logs ms per picture every 100 pictures.

**UI (`isView()` in ui.html)**: `#gal` overlay; card with Visual / "Text in pictures" tabs, Whole word / Contains chips, result grid with score % badge; card states from `GET /api/imgs` (poll 1 s): model missing -> Download (~150 MB); ready -> Scan pictures / Scan new pictures / Rescan all (`clear` then `start force`); running -> bar + Stop; first scan starts after the download. Results open in the viewer (`GCX={dev:'local',path:'/',list}`, items carry full path in `p`); hold-select: Copy, Share, Details. Settings row "Image search model" (uninstall keeps the index). The scan holds a wake lock (`Core.holdAwake`).

**First device test (all OPEN; stop at the first failure)**: (1) CI compiles (likely nits: ORT Java API `OrtSession.Result`, `Tasks.await`, private `Impl`). (2) Download works (404 = fix `FILES`, see 5.11). (3) Self-test passes. (4) Same picture in browser vs phone: cosine > 0.98. (5) Scan ~5k pictures: `msPerPic`, heap. (6) OCR on a Turkish text picture (ç ğ ı ö ş ü); plan B tess-two `tur+eng`. (7) UI end to end incl. viewer swipe and Settings delete model. (8) Then mark this chapter done in section 2.
**Weak points**: per-entry `FloatArray` ~2 KB (50k pictures = ~100 MB heap; switch to one flat array if needed); visual search scans all vectors per query (fine to ~100k); this phone's pictures only; threshold 0.20 / top 60 copied from the web page.

### 11.2 OCR - select text in pictures (image viewer)
**What it does**: viewer **T** button shows a faint box around every word; tap = word, double tap = line, triple tap = paragraph, long press also works; two blue handles with a magnifier (`android.widget.Magnifier`, Android 9+, try / catch); floating toolbar Copy / Search / Share; bottom bar Copy (all) / Select all / Share / Links / Save / X. Pinch, pan, double-tap zoom and page swipe keep working (the overlay claims only touches on a handle or word). Offline after first use. Built on ML Kit (no stable Google Lens API); engine separate from the selection UI; viewer is native Kotlin (ViewPager2 + `ZoomImageView`), so the overlay is a native `View`.
**Contract (do not break without updating all parts)**
```kotlin
data class OcrWord(val text: String, val box: RectF, val quad: FloatArray? = null)   // box in bitmap px; quad = 8 floats clockwise from top-left, only when tilted > ~2 deg
data class OcrLine(val text: String, val box: RectF, val words: List<OcrWord>)
interface OcrEngine { fun recognize(bm: Bitmap, cb: (Result<List<OcrLine>>) -> Unit); fun close() }   // callbacks on the main thread
class OcrOverlayView(c: Context) : View(c) { setImageMatrix(m); setLines(l); selectWordAt(x,y); selectAll(); clearSelection();
    selectedText(); allText(); hasLines(); hasSelection(); var onSelection; var onAction;  /* + hitsWord, tapSelect, touchHint */ }
// ZoomImageView: var onTap, onHold, onMatrixChanged, onTextHit, onTextTap, onTouchAt; fun matrixNow()
```
**Files**
| File | Role |
|---|---|
| `OcrEngine.kt` | contract types + `MlKitOcr` (Play Services module check, download / poll up to 3 min, Block / Line / Element boxes, `quadOf`). Latin model comes from Play Services (`play-services-mlkit-text-recognition:19.0.1`, unbundled; comments saying "bundled" are wrong). Keep ONE copy (it went missing from a zip once) |
| `OcrTiled.kt` | `TiledOcr(base)`: long side >= 2400 px = whole-picture pass + overlapping 1792 px tiles at 1:1 (a tile keeps words centred in its core; tile wins on >= 40 % overlap; `joinRows`); long side < 900 px enlarged 2x; any merge error -> whole-picture result. Constants `BIG, SMALL, TILE, OVER, EDGE, BIGTEXT` |
| `OcrHiRes.kt` | `HiRes`: `BitmapRegionDecoder` on `cacheDir/img/<pos>.bin`, tiles cut from the original when it is >= 1.25x the viewer bitmap (virtual size capped 6144 px), boxes scaled back |
| `PaddleOcr.kt` (**not in the APK**) | `PaddleModels` downloads PP-OCR det + latin rec + dict from `huggingface.co/monkt/paddleocr-onnx` into `filesDir/models/ocr` (Wi-Fi only, on first OCR call); `AutoOcr` = PaddleOCR when the files exist, else ML Kit (2 failures -> files deleted). Viewers and `ClipEngine` create `AutoOcr`. Boxes are axis-aligned; check rec input height, Turkish letters, speed |
| `OcrOverlayView.kt` | transparent view above `ZoomImageView`: word list in reading order, selection = word range, outlines near the finger (all boxes flash ~1.5 s when the layer opens), blue bar per line, handles + magnifier, toolbar; tilted words use polygons |
| `OcrSmart.kt` | Links (link / e-mail / phone pickers), Save menu (text `.txt`, word list `.txt`, copy word list; to `Downloads/picture-text-<timestamp>.txt`), `fold` (case / accents / Turkish i). The unused-code scan lists this file as dead (81 lines): verify before deleting |
| `OcrPrefs.kt` | pref "viewer"/"ocr_auto": 0 always, 1 only while charging, 2 off; `LSAndroid.ocrGet()/ocrSet(m)`; Settings > Text in pictures (`openOcrSettings()`) |

**Viewer (`ImageViewerActivity`)**: automatic reading 0.7 s after a page is shown (`autoOcr()`: current, next, previous; not during the slideshow; follows `OcrPrefs`). **T button states** (`updateOcrBar`): bright = text found / layer on, 0.35 = read, no text, half = reading, 0.8 = not read yet; T shows the layer at once if read, else when done. Long press on the bare picture = T (`holdOcr`). **Disk cache** `cacheDir/ocr/<uuid>.json` (lines, word boxes, `"q"`; valid only for the same bitmap size; `OCR_VER` = **3**; 30 days + 8 MB cap, `pruneOcr`). A failed page is skipped by auto mode, 2 failures in a row stop it; pressing T retries. **Long press on T** = menu: *Find text in these pictures* (`findText` over saved cache JSONs, only pictures already read) / background reading mode. Edit -> replace original drops that page's layer. The same OCR serves the PDF viewer (7.4) and the search index (11.1).
**History**: s9 = build green + device test OK for the basic version; later (tilted text `quad`, full-resolution tiles, cache v3, T long-press menu, Find text, Save menu, Settings page, PaddleOCR + `AutoOcr`) not device-confirmed. Translate was removed 2026-10-10.
**Device checklist**: printed page: T -> boxes; tap / double / triple tap; drag handles (magnifier); Copy -> paste; pinch-zoom keeps handles; next page restores its layer; Turkish `ç ğ ı İ ö ş ü`; first use with no model -> download, then airplane mode works; tilted text (15-30 deg); 12 MP page with small print, no doubled words at tile borders; EXIF-rotated photo keeps boxes; edit -> replace original drops the layer; rotation / split screen keeps alignment.
**Known gaps**: no long-press-and-drag extend (use handles); toolbar / paragraph logic use bounding boxes for strongly rotated text; idea: Settings switch for background reading beyond the three modes.

## 12. Storage Analyzer (+ WizTree view)
### 12.1 Summary and state
WizTree-like analyzer (size tree + treemap), opened by the **storage percentage pill** at the top right of the address bar: inside a folder = analyze that folder (recursive); at the storage root = whole storage. Parts 1-10 are written; compile check done in part 10 (all 81 sources against android-34 `android.jar` with kotlinc 2.0.21: 0 errors in the storage files except missing androidx classes; mirror test `tools/storage_scan_mirror_test.py` 4000 / 4000); **device test still to do**. The WizTree view (12.9), Classic pie zoom and Free up space (12.10) are newer and uncompiled.
**Files**: `core/StorageScan.kt` (engine), `core/StorageStore.kt` (saved results), `core/Volumes.kt`, routes in `core/Routes.kt` + `core/InProc.kt`, `StorageActivity.kt` (screen), `StorageTreemap.kt`, `StorageList.kt`, `StoragePie.kt`, `StorageWiz.kt`, `StorageActions.kt`, `ops/JobOverlay.kt` (hides the scan job), `res/xml/file_paths.xml`. **Hooks**: pill = HTML `#used` (`pl.onclick`, `pillTap()`) and native `NlHead` (pressKind 4 -> `nlOn('used')`); old `spaceInfo()` sheet stays as fallback (archives, old builds); `Jobs` / `DupFinder` were the model for the long job; `Bin` for deletes. Native screens are plain `Activity`s; no new dependencies.

### 12.2 Scan engine (`core/StorageScan.kt`)
```kotlin
StorageScan.start(dir: File, label: String, usedBytes: Long): Scan   // returns at once; 4 daemon threads
StorageScan.get(id) / cancel(id) / latest(dir) / running(dir) / adopt(...) / removeFile / removeNode / pruneNested / deniedBelow
class Scan { state "run|done|cancel|error", error, cancel, files/dirs/bytes (live AtomicLong), denied, deniedSample, cur,
             percent() (-1 unknown, max 99 until done), millis(), root: SNode?, top: List<SNode>, catBytes(), catFiles(), find("/DCIM/Camera"),
             limited, truncated, loaded, removed, job }
class SNode { name, dir, parent, size, files, folders, cat, agg, denied, cats: LongArray?, kids: Array<SNode>, path() }
object SCat { OTHER IMG VID AUD DOC ARC APP, COUNT, KEYS, of(fileName) }
```
- **Memory**: a folder keeps only its 200 largest files as nodes; the rest is ONE synthetic `agg` node (`name ""`, `files` = count) so sums stay exact; empty folders are dropped from `kids` (still counted in `folders`). Node limit per scan = heap / 700 B, 150,000-400,000 (`nodeLimit`), plus a live check (< 32 MB heap left = stop keeping single files, `Scan.truncated`). Memory keeps the newest 3 finished scans and <= 700,000 nodes together.
- **Speed**: one task per folder on a 4-thread pool (breadth first); one `Files.readAttributes(NOFOLLOW_LINKS)` per entry on API 26+; symlinks skipped; sizes are apparent (`length`). Totals filled by a finishing pass (post-order, 16 MB stack thread); `root` / `top` set only when `done`.
- **Unreadable folders** (Android/data on API 30+ without All-files access): `node.denied`, `Scan.denied`, first 20 paths in `deniedSample`. Categories by extension (`SCat.of`): images, video, audio, documents, archives, apps (apk, obb), other; bytes per folder in `SNode.cats`, file counts only for the whole scan.
- **Job**: every scan is a normal `Job` (`sscan:<id>`, label "Analyzing storage" / "Analyzing <folder>"), mirrored ~6 times a second by `StorageScan.mirror`; the job's Cancel sets the cancel flag.
- **Delete patching** (`removeFile` / `removeNode`, synchronized): detach the node, subtract size / files / folders / `cats[]` up the chain, drop emptied ancestors, re-sort, adjust `Scan.top`, `files`, `bytes`, `removed`.

### 12.3 API (all GET in `Routes.api`, helpers `sscan*`)
Category arrays always in `SCat.KEYS` order `other img vid aud doc arc app`. Errors `{error}` + status (404 unknown scan / folder, 400 not finished).
- `/api/sscan?path=/&rescan=0` -> `{id, state, path, whole, error}` (`path=/` = whole main storage with `usedBytes` = total - usable; other path = that folder, percentage -1). Returns the running scan, else the newest finished one, else starts one; `rescan=1` skips the finished one.
- `/api/sscanp?id=` -> `{state, percent, files, dirs, bytes, used, cur, error, ms, denied, keys, catFiles[7]}` (+ when done `catBytes[7], total, folders, deniedSample[]`).
- `/api/sscannode?id=&path=/DCIM&n=60` (n 1..500) -> `{id, path, name, size, files, folders, denied, cats[7], kids[], rest?}`; kid `{n, d, s, f, ...}` (folder adds `fo`, `cats[7]`, `x:true`; file adds `c`; "N small files" = `a:true`, `n:""`); `rest = {count, s, f}`. Paths are scan-relative.
- `/api/sscantop?id=` -> `{id, top:[{n, p, s, c}]}` (100 largest); `/api/sscancancel?id=`. `InProc.ROUTES` has `sscanp`, `sscannode`, `sscantop`.

### 12.4 Screen and views (`StorageActivity.kt`)
- Plain Activity, extra `path` (`/` = whole main storage; `X_VOL` for another volume), `StorageActivity.intent(ctx, vpath)`; own light / dark palette (`AppPal`, same as `JobOverlay.Palette`); process death re-attaches by `path`. Header: back, title, **Rescan** (hidden while scanning); volume card (used bar, Used / Free / Total, red under 5 % free); states: run (percent, bar, counts, current folder, elapsed, **Cancel**; keeps running if you leave), done, cancel / error ("Scan again" + "Show the previous result"). Compact mode (height < 640 dp) hides the volume card.
- `attach()` order: running scan -> newest finished in memory -> saved copy (`StorageStore`, gzip in cache `sscan/`, one file per folder, newest 4, max 7 days, then `StorageScan.adopt`) -> new scan. A result made without All-files access is not reused once access is on.
- **Treemap** (`StorageTreemap`, squarified, flat arrays, cap 20,000 tiles, nesting <= 12; category colours `CAT_COL`; first tap selects, second tap opens / details; `hl` highlights a category). **List** (`StorageList`, hand-drawn rows, `setFilter(cat)`, `setFlat(top)` for **Largest**, multi-select by long press). **Tabs** Treemap | List | Largest (`showIn(n)` jumps to the file's folder). **File-type bar** (`paintCats`, tap = highlight / filter). Back order: select mode -> Largest -> up one folder -> leave. Breadcrumb + info line (Open / Details / File manager / Show).
- **Details dialog**: type, size + exact bytes, % of folder / scan, real path, actions Open, Open with, Share, **Show in file manager** (`MainActivity` `ACTION_SHOW_PATH` + `X_SHOW_PATH`, `runShowPath()` -> `lsGoLocal(p)`; folder only, no highlight), red **Delete...** (not the scan root, not "small files"). Files outside primary storage toast.
- **Delete**: `confirmDelete` (size, counts, bin note `Bin.KEEP_DAYS`, denied-part warning) -> `deleteNode` (`Bin.trash`) -> `afterDeleteMany` patches the tree and refreshes once; multi-select -> `confirmDeleteMany`. Not on other volumes.
- **System / other** = `usedBytes - (root.size + Scan.removed)` with `sysDialog` (Android, app private data / caches, other users, block rounding, Android/data note, unreadable folders, **Allow access**). **All files access**: `needAccess()` = `Core.storageOk == false`, red banner, `Scan.limited`, `onResume` rescans after access is granted.
- **Other volumes** (`core/Volumes.kt`; API 30 `directory`, below hidden `getPath()`, fallback `getExternalFilesDirs`): chips Internal storage + one per volume at the root; volume = view + Open / Open with / Share only. A removed volume falls back to internal.

### 12.7 Not done on purpose
Delete / bin on other volumes, saving a partial scan, a volume picker in the web UI, "System / other" as a treemap block, auto-refresh of a loaded result, multi-select in the treemap, batch share / open, undo, sort options, per-category file counts in the UI, a Main-menu tile (removed 2026-10-11).

### 12.8 Device checklist (all open)
Pill in a folder / at root; progress + Cancel; treemap <-> list; drill in and Back; Largest tab; delete a file -> sizes update without a rescan and the file is in the bin; long-press select several (also in Largest) -> one confirmation; unreadable part warning; no All-files access -> banner, Allow, rescan on return; System / other numbers add up and do not change on delete; SD card / USB chips (Open / Share, no Delete), pull the card -> back to internal; kill the app after a scan -> "Loading the last result..." with age; Rescan hidden while scanning; leave mid-scan -> bubble shows progress and Cancel works; 100k+ files without OOM; rotate / split screen / large text / dark theme. Also look at: screen height at the root with everything stacked, `loadSaved` time for a 400,000-node tree, `StorageVolume.getPath()` on the Vivo phone, whether `Bin.trash` of a folder with an unreadable child works. Compile suspects if CI complains: `hl` custom setter in `StorageTreemap`, `this@StorageList.cat` in `RowView`, `LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, MATCH_PARENT, Float)` in `paintCats`, the `catch (_: IllegalArgumentException)` blocks in `StorageActions`.

### 12.9 WizTree view and Classic pie (2026-10-17 / 19, uncompiled)
- **WizTree view** (`StorageWiz.kt`, default on; pref `storage_ui` `wiz`; "Classic" in the header returns to the old screen): black theme; **Tree View** (+/- expand, Percent / Size / Files), **File View** (filter `*.jpg`, `a|b`, `!x`, `>10mb`, `<1gb`, `/` for path; Max 1000 / 5000 / 20000), **Types** (extension colour, %, size, count; tap = red highlight in the treemap, long-press = filter in File View), cushion treemap by file extension (tap = select, double tap = enter folder, long-press = details), status bar, **CSV export** per tab; landscape = list left, treemap right. Delete from details calls `wiz.refresh()`; Back = parent folder.
- **Treemap zoom** (`WizMap`): pinch 1x-60x, pan when zoomed, double tap on a file / empty space = 2.5x, on a folder = enter; layout recomputed for the visible region only (matrix preview during the gesture, redraw after 160 ms); buttons `－ ＋ 1:1`, `×N` indicator; Back resets zoom first; zoom resets on folder change.
- **Classic screen** (`StoragePie`, donut of the folder's direct children) also zooms (1x-12x, drag to pan, `×N ✕` chip, slices under `MIN_DEG` grouped as "smaller items"). `StorageTreemap.kt` is now used only for `CAT_COL`.
- Gaps: no "Allocated" size; File View sorted by size only; `agg` nodes show as one row in Types; theme fixed dark. Next: compile, check treemap speed (`MAXR = 7000`) and Types colours on the device.

### 12.10 Free up space (cleaner, extended 2026-10-23; uncompiled, no device test)
`core/Cleanup.kt` (`GET/POST /api/cleanup`) + `openFreeUp()` in ui.html (drawer > Free up space). Result keys `free total bin{items,bytes} reclaim{count,bytes}` and lists `big old apk junk empty shots chat` = `{count, bytes, items[{path,size,m,d}]}` (`d` = empty folder).
- **junk**: temp / log / bak / old / chk / dmp / unfinished-download files older than 3 days, everything in `.thumbnails/.thumbs/.cache/.tmp`, `.trashed-*`, `Thumbs.db`, `desktop.ini`, `~$*`, `*~`, `._*`. **empty**: topmost folders without any file (standard top-level folders kept) + 0-byte files. **shots**: Screenshots / Screen recordings older than 30 d. **chat**: WhatsApp / Telegram media older than 90 d and >= 256 KB, also `Android/media/com.whatsapp*|org.telegram*|org.thunderdog*`. **reclaim** = every file of junk / empty / old / apk / shots / chat counted once (big files excluded).
- Page: summary card (free-up total, used bar, **Quick clean** = junk + empty folders in one confirmation, **Analyze storage** -> `LSAndroid.openStorage('/')`), the sections, then Duplicate files / Similar pictures. All deletes go through `/api/rm` (Recycle bin). Open: compile + device test, `.cache` hidden-dir handling, whether `Bin.trash` accepts `Android/media/...`.

### 12.11 Other devices (read-only, 2026-10-21; not compiled / device-tested)
The pill also works on **LANShare phones, SMB, FTP and SFTP** (not Root, not inside archives). `StorageScan.startRemote(dev, vpath, label)` builds the same `SNode` tree; `Scan.remote` / `Scan.dev` mark it and `StorageActivity.ro` (archive or remote) switches off open / share / delete / reveal / multi-select. Results live in memory only (`remoteKey`, never saved).
- **LANShare phone, new build**: the phone scans ITSELF (`/p/sscan`), this phone polls `/p/sscanp` every 0.4 s and downloads the finished tree once with `/p/sscandl` (`StorageStore.encode` / `decode`, gzip); `/p/sscancancel` cancels. **Old build** (404): its `/p/walk` is folded locally (`walkScan`). **SMB**: folder-per-task, 8 parallel `lsFast` calls. **FTP / SFTP**: same on 4 threads (the pool size), depth cap 48, unlistable folder = "unreadable".
- UI: `usedPct()` / `pillSet()` feed the pill (and native `NlHead` `u`) from `/api/space`; `LSAndroid.openStorageDev(dev, name, path)` -> `StorageActivity.intentDev`. No pill where there is no space number (SMB server root, FTP without `space`).
- Limits: nothing can be opened / deleted from a remote scan; speed depends on folder count; no byte-based percentage on folder scans.
## 13. Performance

*Source: Settings > Debug: performance (branch `claude/debug-perf-tab`), reports taken 2026-10-20 on the user's phone. One device, one cold start per build - indicative; force-close and reopen the app before each startup report.*

### 13.1 Cold start (onCreate -> first screen)
| Build | Total | WebView + setup | Server start wait | Page load (ui.html) | Page done -> first screen |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Baseline | 533 ms | 117 | **258** | 102 | 56 |
| Server started early (run 2) | 497 ms | 127 | 46 | **247** | 77 |
| + image engine deferred (run 3, merged) | **335 ms** | 95 | **38** | 157 | 45 |

**Net 533 -> 335 ms (-198 ms, ~-37 %).** `core.url` (server ready) is published 106 ms after process start (baseline `coreReady` 447 ms); 0 long tasks (run 2 had one 65 ms task during page load); `GET /api/folderstat` max 467 ms (baseline 965, run 2 2155); the image engine finishes loading at 981 ms (`core.clipLoaded`), ~590 ms after the first screen, off the critical path.
Marks of the current build (ms since process start): UI thread onCreate 54 -> fontInit 56 -> **webCreated 138 (WebView construction = 82 ms)** -> nativeViews 145 -> contentSet 145 -> permissionsAsked 147 -> loadStart 149. Server thread: threadStart 54 -> core.begin 56 -> cfgLoaded 85 (**29 ms** `Cfg.load`) -> locks 94 -> cleanup 95 -> pageAndFs 99 -> portBound 102 -> discoveryStarted 106 -> **url 106**. Page: loadStart 149 -> coreReady 187 (poll granularity) -> pageFinished 344 (157 ms) -> firstScreen 389.

### 13.2 Findings
- The server is **no longer on the critical path** (ready at 106 ms, before the WebView finishes building at 138 ms). Remaining early cost is WebView construction (82 of the 95 ms).
- Moving the heavy image-search work after `url` first hurt page load (247 vs 102 ms) because model load and watcher ran concurrently with the page load; deferring them until after the first screen fixed most of it (inferred from timings, consistent with run 3).
- Page load is still above baseline (157 vs 102 ms; main script starts at 116 vs 39 ms; DOM ready 152 vs 90 ms). **Unexplained.** Candidates: `WifiPrinters.start` and Discovery threads starting right after `url`; WebView renderer start; run-to-run variance.
- **No jank**: 0 long tasks; frame sample ~90 fps, 0 frames over 33 ms, worst 22 ms (WebView only; the native list is not counted). The UI page is cheap (main script 31-53 ms; ui.html 468 KB, 224 DOM nodes, 10 MB JS heap). Steady state is tiny (~1 s of main-thread time in 387 s: polling intervals, `nlLay`, `pollPeers`).
- Two slow background scans (do not block the UI thread): `GET /api/folderstat` (3 calls, avg 220-850 ms) and `GET /api/wifiprinters` (50-300 ms). Wasteful but cheap: `nlLay` / `nlChPush` / `nlSrPush` ~460 calls each in 8 s; `/api/peers` polled every few seconds.

### 13.3 Design questions answered
| Idea | Verdict |
|---|---|
| Rewrite ui.html fully native | At most ~100 ms of the now 335 ms; huge effort and regression risk. **Not worth it.** (An earlier estimate for a 100 % native UI: ~6,000-9,000 Kotlin lines, 1-2 weeks, no gain for storage reads / SMB / peers.) |
| Minify ui.html | Well under 20 ms. Low value. |
| ui.html growing to 1 MB | Main script run would grow from ~31-53 ms to ~70-120 ms; maintainability is the bigger problem. |
| Split ui.html into ten files | No speed gain; only maintainability. Safe if plain `<script src>` in the same order, startup block last, the server serves the files, debug wrappers installed after all files load. **Not yet checked**: how ui.html is served and whether Kotlin reads / patches it as text. |

### 13.4 Done, and what is open
Done (branches, each stacked on the previous; `claude/defer-image-engine` contains all, merged as PR #66): `claude/debug-perf-tab` (temporary tab, all code tagged `PERF-DEBUG`, `core/PerfMarks.kt`), `claude/faster-server-start` (`Core.start` from the top of `MainActivity.onCreate`, `url` published before the slower setup: server wait 258 -> 38 ms), `claude/startup-marks` (finer marks), `claude/defer-image-engine` (engine + watcher after the first screen: page load 247 -> 157 ms; image search may say "model not loaded" for the first second after launch). `claude/swipe-light-transition` = old, empty diff, close its PR.
Open (ranked) is in section 3 B: (4) why page load is 157 ms, (5) WebView construction, (6) delay the background scans, (7) fewer layout pushes, (8) `Cfg.load` 29 ms (harmless), (9) optional minify / dead code.
**Re-measuring**: Settings > Debug: performance > Refresh / Reset counters / **Record 5 s** (then scroll, swipe, open screens) / Copy. Compare TOTAL onCreate -> first screen, "Page load", "main script starts at", long-task count, API averages.

## 14. Changelog (newest first)

- 2026-10-30 - `phone_explorer.py` now ALWAYS runs as Administrator (Windows): relaunches elevated via UAC, retries once, quits with a message if declined; `--no-admin` ignored.
- 2026-10-30 - Remote shell overlay: z-index 9000 -> 7 (it covered dialogs / the file manager and got mixed up when a zip was opened), and a 300 ms watcher closes it when `S.dev`/`S.path`/`homeOn()` change (user navigated elsewhere). Known: a long silent command (e.g. chkdsk) can drop with 'Software caused connection abort' (idle connection cut over Tailscale); the command may keep running on the PC. Possible follow-up: background job + polling.
- 2026-10-30 - Remote shell Connect sheet: tapping a found PC now fills the Address field with its IP (editing the field drops the selection); the address is what gets used.
- 2026-10-30 - Remote shell screen (`ui.html` `openRemoteShell()`, old `openPcExec()` is an alias): full-screen terminal opened from a Main-menu tile (`sh`) + drawer item; Connect sheet inside it holds PC list (`/api/pcfind` + SMB peers), own address, password, Remember (localStorage `ls_rsh` = {name,ip,port,fromList,rem,pw}, history `ls_rsh_hist`; password only stored when Remember is ticked, else kept in memory `RSH_PW`). Connect test runs `hostname`. PowerShell switch sends `powershell -NoProfile -NonInteractive -EncodedCommand <base64 UTF-16LE>`. Back button closes it (`lsBack`). The Phone Explorer sheet's Remote command button was removed. PC `/exec` timeout 60 -> 300 s, command limit 16000 chars, `_reply` ignores a client that already hung up (that was the ConnectionResetError traceback); `PePc.exec` default timeout 325 s. Commands are never auto-retried (a retry could run e.g. shutdown twice).
- 2026-10-30 - `phone_explorer.py` window: taller (520x760, min 480x720) so the Remote commands section is visible; removed the "Pairing link / Import" row (the phone's QR screen was removed); `--pair` and `import_link` still exist.
- 2026-10-30 - Remote command PC list also includes the app's SMB peers (`/api/peers` kind `SMB`, host taken from `ip`, port 8798, name + via e.g. Tailscale), merged with `/api/pcfind` results; the manual address field stays as fallback. Host parsing of the SMB `ip` string is a guess (strips scheme, path, :port): verify on device.
- 2026-10-30 - Remote command over Tailscale: PC `lan_ip()` already accepts 100.64.0.0/10 (CGNAT, Tailscale); broadcast discovery cannot cross Tailscale, so the Remote command screen got a "PC address" field (Tailscale IP or MagicDNS name, localStorage `ls_pexec_addr`, port 8798); when filled it is used instead of `/api/pcfind`. Windows firewall must allow TCP 8798 on the Tailscale (often "Public") profile. Not compiled / not tested.
- 2026-10-30 - Phone Explorer sheet (`ui.html`) trimmed: removed FTPS + Require FTPS switches, FTPS fingerprint line, Pairing QR block, hint text. Kotlin (`peSet` tls/tlsreq, `peQr`) untouched; the sheet now resets `tls`/`tlsreq` to false on open so no hidden state stays on. Kept: Status/Address/PIN, Open on PC, Remote command, Read-only, Start automatically, Shared folders, Port.
- 2026-10-30 - Remote command now guarded by a PASSWORD (owner request, replaces the no-PIN variant): set in the `phone_explorer.py` window ("Save password", stored as salted PBKDF2 hash `exec_salt`/`exec_hash`; no password = /exec refuses), sent as `pass=` to `/exec`, 5 wrong tries from one IP = 60 s lock. Phone screen has a password field + "Remember password" checkbox (localStorage `ls_pexec_pw_<pc name>`). `PePc.exec(ip,hport,pass,cmd)`, `Routes` `pcexec` pass `pass`. Not compiled.
- 2026-10-30 - Remote command: PIN removed on owner request (see 10.3 update); PC window now has an ON/OFF button instead of the checkbox + PIN box; `PePc.exec`, `Routes` `pcexec`, `ui.html openPcExec` lost the pin. Added CHANGELOG top section. Not compiled.
- 2026-10-30 - Remote command (chapter 10.3), owner's own devices. PC side (`tools/phone_explorer.py`) done + `py_compile`-checked: admin auto-elevation (`relaunch_elevated`, UAC), Task Scheduler autostart (`/RL HIGHEST`), PIN-gated `/exec` (off by default). Phone side coded, NOT compiled: `pe/PePc.kt.exec()`, `Routes.kt` `"pcexec"`, `ui.html` `openPcExec()`. Next session: build APK, device checklist in 10.3.
- 2026-10-29 - USB / OTG drives (chapter 18), NOT compiled (no Android SDK here), not device-tested. New `core/Otg.kt`: removable non-primary volumes (`Volumes.extra`) that look like USB (`isUsb`) become `LocalFs(path, id="otg:<uuid>", name, readOnly)` places, kind `USB` in `/api/peers` (`Routes`), `Jobs.ep` resolves `otg:` ids, `GET/POST /api/otg` = the 3 switches + drive list. `LocalFs` got `id` / `name` / `readOnly` constructor params, `checkWritable()` (called by write, writeResume, mkdir, remove, rename, move, `Jobs` direct `.lspart` write, `ArcEp` archive edit) and `ix()` (name index only for the phone's own storage). **Hazard fixed:** `Jobs.removeUser` sent ANY `LocalFs` delete to `Bin.trash(v)`, which acts on `Core.local` - now only `=== Core.local`; a USB delete is permanent. 3 settings `otg_on` (default true) / `otg_auto` / `otg_ro` in `Cfg` + `SettingsSchema` (SCHEMA 2). `ui.html`: `usb` icon, `usbWatch()` (auto-open on plug-in, back to "This device" on unplug, called from `pollPeers`), `openUsb()` sheet + Settings row, USB excluded from the PC / print pickers. `MainActivity.Bridge.openUsbSettings()` (Eject = Android storage screen). `Core.start` calls `Otg.start` (plug / unplug receivers).
- 2026-10-29 - Lite notification colour, NOT compiled, not device-tested: new `NoteTint.kt` (`NoteTint.lite(ctx)` = `R.color.brand_lite` in lite, 0 in full); every notification builder (`LanShareService.buildNote`, `MainActivity` job notes, `ClipPush`, `PcPrintService` x2, `PeService`) calls `setColor(NoteTint.lite(..))` only when `BuildConfig.LITE`, so full is unchanged. NEW notifications must do the same. If the phone's skin ignores `setColor` (tile stays orange) the small icon itself would need a lite variant.
- 2026-10-29 - Two-APK CI (full + lite) **built green on GitHub Actions, both editions** (owner confirmed). The first run failed in both matrix jobs with `Plugin [id: 'com.android.application'] was not found` at root `build.gradle.kts` line 3 (plugin without a version); fixed by `id("com.android.application") version "8.5.2" apply false` and `id("org.jetbrains.kotlin.android") version "1.9.24" apply false` in the root file (`settings.gradle.kts` `pluginManagement` already lists google / mavenCentral / gradlePluginPortal). CI cost: the matrix runs the two builds in parallel (wall time ~ the slower build) but bills two runners (private repo: ~2x minutes from the monthly quota). Device test of lite still open. **Known risks of the two-APK system (found by code review, not yet seen on a device):** (1) Google Drive sign-in in lite will fail with code 10: the Android OAuth client in 17.2 is registered for package `com.lanshare.app` only; fix = add a second Android OAuth client for `com.lanshare.app.lite` with the same SHA-1 (no code change), or hide Drive in lite. (2) Both apps register the same fixed ports (FTP 2121, UDP 41234 / 41235, MiniHttp): the second one to start fails to bind or answers wrongly; the `PeBoot` receiver (BOOT_COMPLETED, MY_PACKAGE_REPLACED) starts the Phone Explorer service in BOTH apps after a reboot, so a phone with both installed will have a port clash on every boot. (3) The PC side (`pcprint.py`, Phone Explorer discovery) identifies a phone by `Build.MODEL` / address, so two installs on one phone can show up as two identical devices or overwrite each other's pairing. (4) Both manifests declare the same intent filters (VIEW for office / pdf / text / media, SEND, SEND_MULTIPLE, the `.SendShareAlias` and `.PrintSpaceShareAlias` share targets), so Android's chooser lists both apps; labels differ (`${appLabel}`) but a stored "always open with" default may point at the wrong one. (5) `PcPrintService` is exported in both apps with the fixed label "LANShare - print on PC": the phone's print settings show two services with the same name, and the user must enable only one. (6) `UpdateCheck.isNewer` ignores the `-sha` / `-lite` suffix and compares only the numeric tag, so lite and full are always offered the same version; fine, but a lite user whose release lacks the `-lite.apk` asset is sent to the release page (by design). (7) CI: the `release` job needs BOTH builds; if one edition fails, NO release is published for the other either (and the version tag is not consumed, the next run reuses the number). `concurrency` has `cancel-in-progress: false`, so back-to-back pushes queue and each makes a full + lite pair. (8) Data is not shared (own filesDir, prefs, SQLite, FileProvider authority `${applicationId}.fileprovider`), so settings, Universal Search index (a second full index = double storage / battery) and Drive accounts have to be set up twice; the Storage Analyzer Apps tab lists both apps. (9) Every new code path that touches `ai.onnxruntime` must stay behind `BuildConfig.LITE` (see Editions note), otherwise lite crashes with `NoClassDefFoundError` at runtime while CI is still green.

- 2026-10-28 - `pcprint.py` always-on Office, NOT run on Windows (Python parse + protocol test with a stand-in worker only): `OfficeWarm` (`OFFICE`) keeps ONE PowerShell process (`officewarm.ps1`, `PS_WARM`) holding hidden Word / Excel / PowerPoint; requests are one line each (`CONV b64src b64dst`, `CHECK keys`, `QUIT`), answers `OK` / `ERR b64` plus `PIDS` / `LOG` lines. `keeper` thread (started in `serve()`) pings every 3 s: restarts a dead or hung worker (kills it and the Office processes it started), re-opens closed apps, and hands an app over to the user (Visible, released, never closed) when a document of theirs appears in it. `office_to_pdf` order: warm worker, old one-off `PS_OFFICE`, LibreOffice (a warm TIMEOUT skips the one-off). `convert()` hashes the upload and serves `pv-cache` (sha1 + ext, 40 files / 400 MB, LRU). `LANSHARE_NO_WARM=1` turns it off. Device check: first preview after boot, second preview speed, close Word from Task Manager and preview again, double-click a .docx while the service runs (it must appear within ~3 s), password-protected file (180 s timeout then recovery).

- 2026-10-25 - Bug report (owner): extracting a zip stops when the app is minimized, started after the search project. NOT reproduced / root cause NOT confirmed (no phone, no logcat). Read-through: search hooks (`IndexManager.changed`) are non-blocking and never cancel jobs; nothing cancels jobs on pause. Suspect: index / content scan competing for CPU, memory or disk while backgrounded. Mitigation coded (NOT compiled): `IndexManager.powerWait()` now waits while any `Jobs.all` job is running (scans already in progress are not interrupted).
